  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于分块推理的虚拟染色方法在处理千兆像素全切片图像时，无法保持空间连续性，会产生与真实图像严重不匹配的伪影。虽然视觉基础模型（VFM）能提供丰富的特征表示，但其自注意力机制会因全局上下文不同而对同一物理区域产生不一致的特征嵌入（即“上下文污染”问题）。这限制了其在虚拟染色中的应用效果。

Method: 1. 形式化问题：将“上下文污染”问题形式化为层论问题，即VFM嵌入形成的预层违反了胶合公理。
2. 提出SheafStain方法：重新解释VFM特征为层状截面以实现空间和生物上一致的虚拟染色。
3. 框架集成：将类别和分块token集成到薛定谔桥框架中作为层状类别截面。类别token用于锚定生物一致性，分块token形成逐位置的空间图。
4. 模型设计：使用一个在苏木精-伊红（H&E）和免疫组化（IHC）上共同预训练的骨干网络，以产生非退化的跨染色茎，从而通过单一VFM特征空间同时监督输入条件化和输出染色对齐。
5. 评估方法：创新性地在拼接的1024×1024输出图像（而非传统孤立的256×256分块）上进行评估，翻译分辨率为256×256。

Result: 在HER2、ER、PR和Ki-67四种生物标志物的评估中，SheafStain方法在与六种先前方法的对比中表现出有前景的结果，并能有效缓解分块边界的拼接伪影问题。

Conclusion: SheafStain通过将VFM特征重新解释为层状截面并引入薛定谔桥框架，成功解决了虚拟染色中的空间不连续性和“上下文污染”问题。该方法不仅提升了染色准确性，还确保了生成结果的空间连续性和生物一致性，为癌症诊断和预后中的生物标志物量化提供了更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Current virtual staining approaches offer the potential for time- and cost-efficient biomarker quantification in cancer diagnostics and prognostics. However, patch-wise inference for gigapixel whole slide images (WSIs) fails to maintain spatial continuity, yielding artifacts that cause catastrophic mismatches with ground-truth images. Although pathology Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) offer rich representations, their self-attention causes varying global contexts to produce inconsistent embeddings for the same physical region. We formalize and validate this ``context contamination'' as a sheaf-theoretic problem where these embeddings form a presheaf that violates the gluing axiom. To address this, we propose SheafStain, a new approach that reinterprets VFM features as sheaf-like sections for spatially and biologically coherent virtual staining. Specifically, SheafStain integrates class and patch tokens into a Schrödinger Bridge framework as sheaf-like sections. While the class token anchors biological consistency, patch tokens form a per-position spatial map. A backbone co-pretrained on Hematoxylin \& Eosin (H\&E) and Immunohistochemistry (IHC) yields non-degenerate cross-stain stalks, so a single VFM feature space supervises both input conditioning and output stain alignment. Departing from prior work that evaluates on isolated $256 \times 256$ patches and either random-crops or resizes the $1024 \times 1024$ ground truth, we translate at $256 \times 256$ and evaluate on the stitched $1024 \times 1024$ outputs across HER2, ER, PR, and Ki-67. SheafStain demonstrates promising results against six prior methods while mitigating patch-boundary stitching artifacts. Code will soon be released.

</details>


### [59] [Task-Aware Structured Memory for Dynamic Multi-modal In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11853)
*Zhirui Chen,Ziwei Chen,Ling Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TASM，一个无需训练的任务感知结构化记忆框架，通过任务向量引导的压缩、语义感知的令牌合并和分层动态检索，解决了多模态大语言模型中上下文学习的记忆压缩问题，在高压缩比下保持了高性能与适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型依赖上下文学习进行快速任务适配，但其可扩展性受到有限上下文窗口和长多模态序列中键值缓存成本增长的严重限制。现有的内存压缩方法通常依赖于刚性的令牌移除或依赖样本的重要性估计，这引入了偏差，破坏了语义结构（尤其是视觉表示），并产生了无法适应新查询的静态记忆。

Method: TASM是一个无需训练的框架，包含三个核心部分：1) 任务向量引导压缩：用捕捉跨演示共享相关性的任务级方向取代样本特定信号；2) 语义感知令牌合并：通过二部图匹配聚合令牌，在不破坏性裁剪的情况下保持底层流形；3) 结构化分层记忆：将记忆构建为包含紧凑核心记忆和潜在库的层次结构，以支持查询自适应的动态检索。

Result: 评估证实，TASM在高压缩比下仍能保持高性能，有效地平衡了效率与适应性。

Conclusion: TASM通过任务感知、结构保持和动态可访问的记忆构建，有效解决了现有内存压缩方法的局限性，为多模态大语言模型在长序列任务中的高效上下文学习提供了可扩展且适应性强的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) depend on in-context learning (ICL) for rapid task adaptation, but their scalability is severely limited by finite context windows and the growing cost of key-value (KV) caches in long multi-modal sequences. Existing memory compression approaches typically rely on rigid token removal or sample-dependent importance estimation, which introduces bias, disrupts semantic structure, particularly for visual representations, and yields static memories that cannot adapt to new queries. We introduce TASM (Task-Aware Structured Memory), a training-free framework that addresses these limitations through task-aware, structure-preserving, and dynamically accessible memory construction. TASM employs task-vector guided compression to replace sample-specific signals with a task-level direction that captures shared relevance across demonstrations. To preserve the underlying manifold, it applies semantics-aware token merging via bipartite graph matching, aggregating tokens without destructive pruning. Finally, TASM structures memory into a hierarchy comprising a compact Core Memory and a Latent Bank, facilitating query-adaptive dynamic retrieval. Evaluations confirm TASM maintains high performance under heavy compression, effectively balancing efficiency with adaptability.

</details>


### [60] [SG2Loc: Sequential Visual Localization on 3D Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11880)
*Nicole Damblon,Olga Vysotska,Federico Tombari,Marc Pollefeys,Daniel Barath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于3D场景图的轻量级顺序视觉定位方法，通过紧凑的场景图表示和粒子滤波框架，显著降低了存储需求，同时在室内定位任务中保持了高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉定位方法通常需要存储大量图像数据库或点云，导致存储开销巨大。本文旨在开发一种轻量级方法，在减少存储需求的同时，维持复杂室内环境中的定位精度。

Method: 方法采用3D场景图表示环境，节点为带有粗略网格的物体，边编码空间关系。定位阶段从图像中提取逐块语义特征预测物体身份。使用粒子滤波框架，每个粒子代表一个相机姿态，将场景图中的物体网格投影到图像，根据可见性分配物体身份。通过比较图像块特征与场景图物体特征计算粒子权重，并顺序整合后续图像以优化姿态估计。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上，该方法显著减少了存储需求，同时保持了与传统方法相当的定位性能，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 基于3D场景图的顺序视觉定位方法提供了一种高效的轻量级替代方案，有效平衡了存储开销与定位精度，适用于机器人学和增强现实等存储受限的应用场景。

Abstract: Visual localization in complex indoor environments remains a critical challenge for robotics and AR applications. Sequential localization, where pose estimates are refined over time, is important for autonomous agents. However, traditional methods often require storing extensive image databases or point clouds, leading to significant overhead. This paper introduces a novel, lightweight approach to sequential visual localization using 3D scene graphs. Our method represents the environment with a compact scene graph, where nodes represent objects (with coarse meshes) and edges encode spatial relationships. For each image in the localization phase, we extract per-patch semantic features, predicting object identities. Localization is performed within a particle filter framework. Each particle, representing a camera pose, projects the coarse object meshes from the scene graph into the image, assigning object identities to patches based on visibility. The similarity of the per-patch features, in the input image, and object features from the scene graph determines the weight of a particle. Subsequent images are incorporated sequentially, refining the pose estimate. By leveraging a compact scene graph and efficient semantic matching, our method significantly reduces storage while maintaining performance on real-world datasets. The code will be available at https://github.com/DmblnNicole/sg2loc.

</details>


### [61] [Image Quality Assessment of Identity Cards Using Measures from Open Face Image Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11884)
*Gregor Grote,Juan E. Tapia,Christian Rathgeb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究将OFIQ质量评估标准应用于远程验证中的身份证图像，通过预处理流程和性能相关性分析，证明基于OFIQ的质量度量可以提升呈现攻击检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决远程验证系统中身份证图像质量评估的挑战。

Method: 应用OFIQ标准中的捕获相关质量度量到身份证图像，进行角点检测、透视校正和前景掩模等预处理，并通过分析这些度量与三种PAD算法在四个数据集（包含真实图像和印刷假卡）上的性能相关性来评估有效性。

Result: 基于某些OFIQ度量的质量评估可以显著提高PAD算法的性能。

Conclusion: OFIQ质量评估标准适用于身份证图像质量评估，并能有效提升呈现攻击检测性能。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of assessing image quality in ID cards in remote verification systems by applying capture-related quality measures from the Open Face Image Quality (OFIQ) standard to ID card images. Our preprocessing pipeline includes corner detection, perspective normalization, and comprehensive foreground masking to ensure accurate and unbiased quality measure computation. We evaluate the effectiveness of these measures by analyzing their correlation with the performance of three presentation attack detection (PAD) algorithms across four diverse ID card datasets, where two datasets contain bona fide, i.e. pristine, images and two contain printed mock ID cards. Our results suggest that quality assessment based on some OFIQ measures can significantly improve PAD performance.

</details>


### [62] [Wild3R: Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting from Unconstrained Sparse Photo Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11894)
*Yuto Furutani,Takashi Otonari,Kaede Shiohara,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Wild3R，一种用于处理不受约束稀疏照片集的前馈3D高斯溅射方法，通过构建新的WildCity数据集解决了光照多样性和瞬态物体带来的挑战，性能超越现有前馈方法并接近逐场景优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式3D高斯溅射方法在处理现实世界照片集时效果不佳，因为缺乏包含多视角、多样光照条件和瞬态变化的大规模训练数据，导致模型难以学习鲁棒的场景表示。

Method: 1. 构建WildCity数据集：包含200个场景、170种光照条件和瞬态物体，共337,500张图像。2. 训练前馈模型：利用该数据集使模型学习在参考视图条件下跨视图的外观一致性，同时能够去除瞬态内容。

Result: 实验证明，该方法在性能上优于现有的前馈式3D高斯溅射方法，并与基于逐场景优化的传统方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: Wild3R成功解决了前馈式3D高斯溅射在处理现实世界照片集时的挑战，证明了大规模、多样化数据集对于学习鲁棒场景表示的重要性，为无约束图像集合的3D重建提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) removes the need for time-consuming per-scene optimization required by traditional 3DGS. However, existing feed-forward approaches struggle with real-world photo collections that include diverse lighting conditions and transient objects. In this paper, we present Wild3R, a feed-forward approach for unconstrained sparse photo collections. The main bottleneck is the lack of training data that provides multiple viewpoints, a variety of illuminations, and transient variations necessary for learning robust scene representations. To address this, we introduce the WildCity dataset, which comprises 200 scenes, 170 lighting conditions, and transient objects, resulting in 337,500 images in total. By leveraging the dataset, our model learns appearance consistency across viewpoints conditioned on reference views, while removing transient content. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms existing feed-forward approaches and achieves results competitive with prior per-scene optimization-based methods.

</details>


### [63] [From Content to Knowledge: Lightning Fast Long-Video Understanding with Neural Knowledge Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11913)
*Yuchen Guan,Xiao Li,Zongyu Guo,Xiaoyi Zhang,Xiulian Peng,Chun Yuan,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这篇论文提出了一种新的长视频理解范式，将视频内容编码为附加在视觉语言模型上的轻量级神经知识表示，实现了高效的多轮视频理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解面临计算成本高、内存需求大等挑战，现有方法通常需要处理整个视频流或构建复杂数据库，推理效率较低。需要一种能解耦视频长度与推理成本的方法，以支持高效的交互式长视频理解。

Method: 提出神经知识表示（NKR）方法，通过智能体知识蒸馏（AKD）过程将视频语义内容编码到附加在VLM骨干网络的小权重中。AKD阶段由智能体自动合成密集描述和问答对，将视频知识蒸馏到NKR中。推理时将轻量级NKR加载到冻结的VLM上，实现基于查询的直接理解，无需重新加载或编码原始视频。

Result: 在LVBench基准上的实验表明，该方法性能与最先进方法相当，同时将端到端延迟降低了两个数量级以上，显著提高了推理效率。

Conclusion: 该方法为交互式长视频理解开辟了新途径，通过将视频转化为可移植、可重用的资产，实现了高效的多轮视频理解，具有高摊销效率。

Abstract: We propose a new paradigm for long video understanding by treating a long video as a Neural Knowledge Representation (NKR). NKR represents video contents neither as a stream of tokens nor pre-organized databases, but as an individual small portion of network weights attached to the VLM backbone. The NKR weights are optimized to encapsulate the video's semantic content via a novel Agentic Knowledge Distillation (AKD) process, where an agent automatically synthesizes dense descriptions and question-answer pairs to distill the video's knowledge into the NKR. While AKD serves as a comprehensive, one-time encoding phase, the resulting NKR transforms the video into a portable, reusable asset. At inference, the lightweight NKR is mounted onto a frozen Vision-Language Model (VLM), enabling direct, query-based understanding without reloading or re-encoding the original video. This approach decouples video length from inference cost, offering high amortized efficiency for multi-turn video understanding. Experiments on the LVBench benchmark show our method achieves performance comparable to state-of-the-art approaches while reducing end-to-end latency by over two orders of magnitude, opening new possibilities for interactive long-video understanding.

</details>


### [64] [Corpus Augmentation for Sign Language Translation via LLM-Guided Video Stitching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11925)
*Zsolt Robotka,Ádám Rák,Jalal Al-Afandi,András Horváth,György Cserey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对手语翻译的数据增强方法，通过利用现有带注解的语料库和大型语言模型生成新的句子对，无需额外人工标注或外部数据，有效提升了模型性能，解决了高质量并行数据稀缺的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是为了解决手语翻译中高质量并行数据稀缺的瓶颈问题，提升模型在长尾词汇和未见句子结构上的泛化能力，同时避免依赖额外的人工标注或外部资源。

Method: 提出一种语料库增强方法：首先从训练视频中通过CTC强制对齐提取每个词汇片段；然后利用基于语料库的大型语言模型生成新的词汇-句子对；最后通过随机句子采样和片段分配组装成合成序列。生成的合成RGB视频-文本对与下游架构无关，可直接用于基于RGB的手语翻译模型，或通过管道转换为姿态或特征表示。

Result: 在相同的评估框架下，与仅提升0.98 BLEU-4的基线方法相比，本文的增强方法实现了+2.92 BLEU-4的显著提升，且无需改变模型架构或训练协议。研究还发现，合成数据在视觉-语言预训练阶段会损害性能，尽管其提升了预训练目标；此外，优化片段过渡以实现视觉平滑性在L2标准下适得其反，可能因为突变边界起到了隐式正则化作用。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于语料库和大型语言模型的数据增强方法有效缓解了手语翻译中数据稀缺的问题，显著提升了模型性能。研究揭示了合成数据在预训练阶段的潜在负面影响以及视觉过渡优化的反直觉效果，为未来研究提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Sign language translation (SLT) converts sign language video into spoken language text and holds significant promise for improving accessibility and enabling communication between signing and non-signing communities. While large weakly-aligned datasets have enabled pre-training at scale and gloss-free methods have reduced reliance on expert annotation, high-quality parallel sign video-text pairs for fine-tuning remain scarce, limiting generalisation on long-tail vocabulary and unseen constructions. We propose a corpus augmentation approach that requires no additional human annotation, external sign-language video corpora, or generative video models, relying only on the existing gloss-annotated training corpus and an LLM for sentence generation: per-gloss clips are extracted from training videos via CTC forced-alignment, novel gloss-sentence pairs are generated by a corpus-anchored LLM, and synthetic sequences are assembled through random sentence sampling and clip assignment. The resulting synthetic RGB video-text pairs are architecture-agnostic at the downstream training stage and can be consumed directly by RGB-based SLT models, or converted into pose or feature representations by pipelines that derive such inputs from video. Sincan et al. re-evaluated five recent gloss-free methods under strictly identical conditions; the largest verified gain over the GFSLT-VLP baseline was only 0.98 BLEU-4. Our augmentation, applied within the same framework, achieves +2.92 BLEU-4 without any change to architecture or training protocol. We further identify that synthetic data harms vision-language pretraining despite improving its objectives, and that optimising clip transitions for visual smoothness is counter-productive under L2-based criteria; we propose that abrupt boundaries may act as a form of implicit regularisation. Code is available at https://github.com/robizso/slt-datagen.

</details>


### [65] [Feature extraction for plant growth estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11966)
*Simbarashe Aldrin Ngorima,Albert Helberg,Marelie H. Davel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文比较了用于植物生长阶段估计的两种特征提取方法：基于Gabor滤波器和形态学操作的手工特征，以及基于预训练CNN和迁移学习的特征。在油菜和萝卜数据集上的实验表明，CNN方法在速度和准确性上均优于手工特征方法，最佳系统达到98.4%准确率和0.08秒/图的处理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准农业需要实时估计植物生长阶段以优化资源利用，减少浪费。然而，植物在不同生长阶段具有相似的形态特征，这给自动估计带来了挑战。因此，研究动机是开发更准确、高效的特征提取方法，以实现可靠、实时的生长阶段识别。

Method: 研究提出了两种特征提取方法：1）使用Gabor滤波器和形态学操作提取手工特征；2）使用预训练卷积神经网络（如VGG-19）和迁移学习提取特征。实验在公开数据集bccr-segset上进行，针对油菜和萝卜两种室内生长的植物。分类器采用支持向量机（包括径向基函数核）和提升树，对两种方法进行对比评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于CNN的特征在速度和准确性上均优于手工特征。最佳系统采用VGG-19特征和径向基函数支持向量机，在油菜和萝卜数据集上均达到98.4%的分类准确率，每张图像处理时间仅为0.08秒，证明其适用于实时应用。

Conclusion: 基于预训练CNN和迁移学习的特征提取方法在植物生长阶段估计任务中表现出色，比传统手工特征方法更准确、更快速，且具备实时处理能力。这表明深度学习技术可有效应用于精准农业中的自动化生长阶段监测。

Abstract: Precision agriculture requires the estimation of plant growth stages in real-time. When the plant growth stage is known, the wastage of resources in cultivation, such as nutrients and water, is reduced as only the required resources need to be supplied. Plants at different growth stages, however, have similar morphological features, which can make autonomous growth stage estimation difficult. This paper presents two feature extraction methods for growth stage estimation: one that uses a bank of Gabor filters and morphological operations, and the other that uses pre-trained convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and transfer learning. We test these methods on a publicly available plant growth stage dataset (``bccr-segset``) for two species, canola and radish, grown and captured under indoor conditions. The two proposed feature extraction methods are compared, using support vector machines and boosted trees as classifiers. We find that both methods are suitable for real-time applications, and that CNN features outperform the hand-crafted features, both with regard to speed and accuracy. The best system (VGG-19 features, classified with a radial basis function support vector machine) obtained an accuracy of 98.4% for both species, processing an image in 0.08 seconds.

</details>


### [66] [SpecLoR: Spectral Lookahead Rectification for Motion-Coherent Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11969)
*Xu Zhang,Yu Lu,Ruijie Quan,Zhaozheng Chen,Bohan Wang,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpecLoR是一种即插即用的推理方法，通过前瞻预测跳过噪声并将校正转移到频域，利用自然视频的通用统计先验来解决视频生成中的时空不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow Matching方法中的速度近似和数值离散化误差会累积，导致采样轨迹漂移，生成的视频存在严重的时空不一致性。直接校正漂移的噪声潜在变量面临两个挑战：时步相关的噪声掩盖了可靠的结构线索；空间干预会破坏复杂的局部几何并产生高计算成本。

Method: SpecLoR在早期采样阶段通过前瞻预测估计干净的潜在变量z_{t,0}，计算其3D时空频谱，然后校正幅度频谱以匹配自然视频的先验统计特性，保持相位不变，最后将校正后的状态重新加噪以继续ODE积分。

Result: 在Wan2.2上的实验表明，SpecLoR显著减少了物理伪影并增强了运动一致性，在多个基准测试中表现良好，且计算开销最小（仅增加4次NFE）。

Conclusion: SpecLoR通过频域校正和前瞻预测机制，有效解决了视频生成中的时空不一致问题，在保持计算效率的同时提高了生成视频的质量。

Abstract: Flow Matching has enabled robust text-to-video generation via latent ODE sampling. However, velocity approximation and numerical discretization errors inevitably accumulate, causing sampling trajectories to drift. Consequently, generated videos often suffer from severe spatiotemporal inconsistencies. Nevertheless, directly correcting these drifted, noisy latents is challenging: (i) timestep-dependent noise obscures reliable structural cues; (ii) spatial interventions risk disrupting intricate local geometry while incurring heavy computational costs. To address this, we propose Spectral Lookahead Rectification (SpecLoR), a plug-and-play inference method that bypasses noise via lookahead prediction, and circumvents spatiotemporal entanglement by shifting corrections to the frequency domain, where universal statistical priors of natural videos are readily available. First, during early sampling stages, SpecLoR looks ahead to estimate the clean latent $z_{t,0}$ and computes its 3D spatiotemporal spectrum. Next, SpecLoR rectifies the amplitude spectrum to match the prior, leaving the phase intact. Finally, the corrected state is re-noised to resume ODE integration. Experiments on Wan2.2 demonstrate that SpecLoR significantly reduces physical artifacts and enhances motion coherence across multiple benchmarks with minimal computational overhead (4 additional NFEs).

</details>


### [67] [ParseFixer: An Agentic Framework for Document Parsing via Selective Multimodal Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11977)
*LeKai Yu,Hao Liu,Kun Wang,Zhiran Li,Ruping Cao,Fan Liu,Yupeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为ParseFixer的代理式框架，用于解决文档解析挑战，旨在从页面图像中恢复结构化Markdown文档。该框架通过骨干解析和选择性校正相结合，在挑战赛中获得第三名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战赛要求从文档图像中准确恢复文本内容和文档结构，这需要模型同时满足内容准确性和结构忠实性两个互补的需求。

Method: 框架包含两个核心模块：1. 全页面骨干解析（FBP）：使用MinerU2.5 Pro生成稳定的初始Markdown输出；2. 代理式选择性校正（ASC）：检测高价值的解析失败，并通过“验证-回滚”的校正过程进行修复。该方法将选择性多模态校正置于开源骨干解析之后，旨在改进关键文档元素的恢复而不覆盖可靠的骨干预测。

Result: 在测试集上，最终系统的总体得分为61.78，在Track 1（文档解析）中排名第三，证明了该方法在准确文档解析方面的有效性。

Conclusion: ParseFixer框架通过结合骨干解析与选择性校正的策略，有效提升了文档解析任务中关键元素的恢复精度，在DataMFM挑战赛中取得了有竞争力的成绩。

Abstract: In this report, we present our third-place solution for the DataMFM Challenge Track 1: Document Parsing. This track requires models to recover structured Markdown documents from document page images while preserving textual content and document structure. To address the complementary requirements of accurate content recovery and faithful structure reconstruction, we propose ParseFixer, an agentic framework for backbone parsing and selective correction. ParseFixer consists of two key modules: Full-Page Backbone Parsing (FBP) and Agentic Selective Correction (ASC). FBP produces stable initial Markdown outputs with MinerU2.5 Pro, while ASC detects high-value parsing failures and repairs them through a verify-and-rollback correction process. By placing selective multimodal correction after open-source backbone parsing, ParseFixer improves the recovery of key document elements without rewriting reliable backbone predictions. On the test set, our final system achieves an overall score of 61.78 and ranks third in Track 1, demonstrating its effectiveness for accurate document parsing. Our code will be released at: https://github.com/iLearn-Lab/CVPRW26-ParseFixer.

</details>


### [68] [From Nominal Intensity to Equivalent Rainfall: A Path-Based Credibility Evaluation Framework for Simulated Rainfall in Autonomous-Driving Perception Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11989)
*Tian Xia,Xin Zhao,Shaolingfeng Ye,Junyi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于路径的自动驾驶感知测试中模拟降雨可信度评估方法，通过结合雨滴谱真实性和激光雷达感知一致性，优选出能代表真实降雨条件的测试路径，以支持更可信的自动驾驶雨天场景评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶封闭场地测试中，对模拟降雨条件的描述通常仅依赖于标称降雨强度或单点测量，这导致模拟雨场难以与真实降雨对齐，测试结果也难以直接映射到真实世界场景。为了进行基于预期功能安全（SOTIF）的风险评估，需要一种能够量化模拟降雨可信度的方法，特别是其对真实降雨下感知系统性能的模拟保真度。

Method: 本文提出了一种基于路径的可信度评估方法。首先，以真实降雨的雨滴尺寸-速度联合分布为参考，使用路径等效降雨强度、不确定性带和路径平均雨滴分布真实感分数来表征候选测试路径。其次，引入激光雷达目标点云计数和平均反射率进行感知一致性校正，量化每条模拟降雨路径对真实降雨感知效果的代理能力。该方法在约10,000个真实降雨雨滴谱样本、728个RainSense感知样本以及在一个2.4米×7.2米模拟降雨区域内45个空间采样点的数据上进行了验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，在相同标称条件下，模拟降雨场的空间非均匀性依然显著，证实了基于路径评估的必要性。通过该方法，识别出Path IV和Path VI为优选候选路径，它们的评估结果分别为11.54 ± 0.31 mm/h (RRD = 0.43) 和 8.28 ± 0.34 mm/h (RRD = 0.46)。这些路径在降雨强度稳定性、雨滴谱真实性和感知一致性方面表现更为均衡。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于路径的可信度评估方法，为自动驾驶雨天感知测试提供了有效的路径选择、条件描述和结果可信度解释支持。它通过量化模拟降雨的物理和感知双重保真度，有助于将封闭场地测试结果更可靠地推广到真实世界驾驶场景。

Abstract: Credible simulated-rainfall conditions are essential for identifying perception-system boundaries and supporting SOTIF-oriented risk assessment in automated driving. However, closed-field tests are often described only by nominal rainfall intensity or single-point measurements, making it difficult to align simulated rain fields with real rainfall and map test results to real-world scenarios. This paper proposes a path-based credibility evaluation method for simulated rainfall in autonomous-driving perception tests. Using the drop size and velocity joint distribution of real rainfall as the reference, each candidate path is represented by path-equivalent rainfall intensity, an uncertainty band, and a path-averaged Realism of Raindrop Distribution (RRD) score. Lidar target point-cloud count and mean reflectivity are further used for perception-consistency correction, quantifying the proxy capability of each simulated-rainfall path for real-rainfall perception effects. Experiments are conducted using about 10,000 real-rainfall raindrop-spectrum samples, 728 RainSense perception samples, and 45 spatial sampling points in a 2.4 m x 7.2 m simulated-rainfall area. Results show that spatial non-uniformity remains under the same nominal condition, confirming the need for path-based evaluation. The method identifies Path IV and Path VI as preferable candidates, with results of 11.54 +/- 0.31 mm/h, RRD = 0.43, and 8.28 +/- 0.34 mm/h, RRD = 0.46, respectively. These paths show more balanced performance in rainfall-intensity stability, raindrop-spectrum realism, and perception consistency. The proposed method supports path selection, condition description, and credible interpretation of autonomous-driving perception tests under rainfall.

</details>


### [69] [FitVTON: Fit-aware Virtual Try-On via Body-Garment Size Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12012)
*Yiqun Ning,Ao Shen,Chenhang He,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FitVTON是一个感知合身效果的虚拟试穿模型，通过编码服装-身体尺寸信息、使用模拟数据训练和引入纹理校正等技术，在保持图像质量的同时，显著提升了虚拟试穿在不同体型上的合身真实感和尺寸准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于扩散的虚拟试穿方法主要将任务视为2D图像修补，优先考虑纹理保留而非物理合理性。这导致生成的图像虽然视觉上看似合理，但无法真实反映服装在不同体型上的合身效果，缺乏实际服装尺码对应的真实性。

Method: 1. 通过结构化文本提示编码服装与身体的尺寸关系。2. 利用参数化服装模型生成模拟试穿三元组（人物、服装、合身效果）作为训练数据。3. 引入两个辅助预测头，分别预测服装和暴露身体的掩码，以优化服装轮廓的合身效果。4. 引入一个纹理校正阶段，改善从模拟数据中生成图像的真实外观。5. 构建了一个名为FittingEffect3K的真实世界数据集，并设计了基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的评分协议用于评估。

Result: 主观和定量实验均表明，FitVTON展示了真实的合身保真度。在尺寸准确性和形状保持方面显著优于现有最先进的方法，同时保持了有竞争力的图像质量。

Conclusion: FitVTON通过显式建模服装与身体的尺寸关系、利用模拟数据进行训练以及优化合身效果与纹理，有效解决了虚拟试穿中物理合理性不足的问题，为在真实世界多样化体型上实现高保真度的虚拟试穿提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: While diffusion-based virtual try-on has achieved impressive visual realism, most methods treat the task as 2D inpainting, prioritizing texture preservation over physical plausibility. Consequently, they often produce plausible-looking images that fail to reflect authentic garment fit across diverse body shapes. We present FitVTON, a Fit-aware virtual try-on model on different bodies in the wild. FitVTON encodes garment-body size through structured text prompts, and learn from simulated try-on triplets from parameterized garment model. To improve the fitting effects over garment silhouettes, we introduce two auxiliary head to predict the masks for both the garment and the exposed body. We further introduce a texture rectification stage to improve realistic appearance from simulated data. To evaluate the fitting fidelity, we curate a real-world dataset, FittingEffect3K, combining VLM-based scoring protocol. Both subjective and quantitive experiments show that FitVTON demonstrate authentic fitting fidelity, with significant sizing accuracy and shape preservation over state-of-the-art methods while maintaining competitive image quality. Project Page: https://zenoning.github.io/FitVTON/.

</details>


### [70] [ViT-FREE: Efficient Face Recognition via Early Exiting and Synthetic Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12023)
*Tahar Chettaoui,Guray Ozgur,Eduarda Caldeira,Naser Damer,Fadi Boutros*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ViT-FREE，一种用于人脸识别的视觉Transformer多退出推理框架，可在不修改或重训主干模型的情况下，通过中间层特征直接进行人脸验证，显著降低推理成本。实验表明，在层10退出可在保持高准确率的同时获得20%的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉Transformer (ViT) 在人脸识别领域展现出强大潜力，但其高昂的计算成本使其在资源受限的设备上部署面临挑战。因此，需要一种能够平衡效率与准确性的方法。

Method: 本文引入ViT-FREE，这是一个多退出框架。它利用ViT各层特征维度一致的特点，允许直接从中间层的表示（如patch嵌入和注意力图）进行人脸验证，而无需修改或重训练主干模型。此外，提出了ViT-FREE_FT轻量级微调策略，通过小型合成数据集仅微调投影层，以提升浅层退出的性能。

Result: 实验表明，ViT各层特征具有渐进性，连续层间相似度高且与最终表示的对齐度逐渐增加，证明中间层已具备稳定且有区分性的表示。系统分析显示，在IJB-C等基准上，从第10层退出可实现高达20%的速度提升，而验证性能仅下降1.5个百分点，展现了优异的精度-效率权衡。轻量级微调策略成功提升了浅层退出的性能。

Conclusion: ViT-FREE为资源受限场景下的高效人脸识别推理提供了一种简单有效的训练无关策略。它证明了预训练ViT的中间层特征已足够用于准确的人脸验证，通过在合适的深度（如第10层）早期退出，可以在几乎不损失精度的情况下显著提升效率。轻量级微调方案进一步优化了该框架的实用性。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) have gained significant attention in computer vision and shown strong potential for face recognition (FR). However, their high computational cost makes deployment on resource-constrained devices challenging, motivating the need for methods that balance efficiency and accuracy. In this work, we investigate early exiting in pretrained ViTs as a simple yet effective training-free strategy for efficient FR inference. Leveraging the uniform feature dimensionality across transformer encoder blocks, we introduce ViT-FREE, a multi-exit framework that enables face verification directly from intermediate representations without modifying or retraining the backbone model, and thus, reducing inference cost. Empirically, we show that patch embeddings and attention maps evolve progressively across depth, exhibiting high similarity between consecutive ViT blocks and increasing alignment with the final representation. This indicates gradual feature refinement and attention convergence, suggesting that intermediate layers already provide stable and discriminative representations suitable for early exiting. Through extensive experiments on multiple FR benchmarks, we systematically analyze the accuracy-efficiency trade-off across exit depths. Our results demonstrate that later exits achieve a highly favorable balance, with exiting at layer 10 yielding up to a 20% speedup while incurring only a 1.5 drop in verification performance on benchmarks such as IJB-C. Also, we propose ViT-FREE_FT, a lightweight exit-specific fine-tuning strategy that adapts only the projection layers using a small synthetic dataset while keeping the transformer backbone frozen. This approach improves the performance of shallow exits while preserving the efficiency benefits and leaving deeper exits largely unaffected.

</details>


### [71] [SpikeTAD: Spiking Neural Networks for End-to-End Temporal Action Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12033)
*Min Yang,Mi Zhou,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个基于脉冲神经网络（SNN）的端到端时序动作检测（TAD）架构SpikeTAD，在保持极低功耗的同时，在THUMOS14和ActivityNet-1.3数据集上取得了具有竞争力的mAP性能，验证了SNN在低功耗视频理解任务上的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频理解模型在移动设备上的部署面临模型体积大、功耗高的挑战。脉冲神经网络（SNN）因其生物合理性和低功耗特性（尤其适合神经形态芯片）被视为有潜力的解决方案，但其应用受限于转换时间步长过长和性能下降严重的问题。因此，本研究旨在探索SNN在关键的视频理解任务——时序动作检测（TAD）中的应用，并解决上述挑战。

Method: 提出了首个基于SNN的端到端TAD架构SpikeTAD。该架构旨在解决SNN在TAD任务中面临的转换效率和性能瓶颈问题，专门设计用于实现低功耗且有效的时序动作检测。

Result: SpikeTAD在THUMOS14数据集上取得了67.2%的平均mAP，在ActivityNet-1.3数据集上取得了37.42%的mAP。这些结果在保持模型极低功耗的前提下实现，证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究成功将SNN应用于时序动作检测任务，提出的SpikeTAD架构验证了构建低功耗、高性能视频理解模型的可行性。这为未来在移动设备等资源受限平台上部署视频理解应用提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Video understanding is a crucial part of computer vision, with numerous application scenarios. With the increasing popularity of mobile devices, an increasing number of efforts are trying to deploy video understanding models on them. However, existing video understanding models are difficult to deploy due to their large size and prohibitive power consumption. Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) have shown bioplausibility and low power advantages over Artificial Neural Networks (ANNs), especially on neuromorphic chips which are regarded as essential components of future mobile devices. However, excessively long conversion time-steps and severe performance degradation problems limit their application. To solve the problems above, we explore the application of SNNs on temporal action detection (TAD), which is an important task in video understanding, and propose the first SNN-based end-to-end TAD architecture coined as SpikeTAD. While maintaining extremely low power consumption, SpikeTAD achieves an average mAP of 67.2% in THUMOS14 and 37.42% in ActivityNet-1.3, demonstrating the feasibility of a low-power TAD model. Our code is available at https://github.com/MCG-NJU/SpikeTAD.

</details>


### [72] [Vision Transformers for Face Recognition Need More Registers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12036)
*Tahar Chettaoui,Guray Ozgur,Eduarda Caldeira,Naser Damer,Fadi Boutros*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对人脸识别中Vision Transformers（ViT）的拼接补丁嵌入（CPE）方法在注意力图中存在伪影、可解释性差的问题，提出在初始补丁嵌入中引入可学习的寄存器标记（register tokens），与补丁一同通过ViT编码器。实验表明，该方法能有效消除伪影，生成更结构化、可解释的注意力图，并在IJB-B/C基准上达到先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人脸识别中基于ViT的拼接补丁嵌入（CPE）方法虽性能优于标准CLS标记方法，但其注意力图中存在明显的伪影，严重限制了模型的可解释性，不利于理解模型决策过程。

Method: 提出在CPE框架中引入寄存器标记（register tokens）。具体做法是将若干可学习的寄存器标记与初始图像补丁嵌入拼接在一起，共同输入ViT编码器进行处理。通过调整寄存器数量（如4或8个）并分析其对注意力图结构和识别性能的影响。

Result: 1. 实验证明注意力图伪影在不同规模的ViT骨干网络中普遍存在。2. 引入寄存器标记能有效消除这些伪影，生成更平滑、结构化的注意力图，显著提升可解释性。3. 使用8个寄存器标记的模型（ViT-8R）在可解释性与识别准确率上均表现最优，在IJB-B和IJB-C大规模基准测试中达到了最先进的验证准确率。

Conclusion: 寄存器标记机制是提升基于CPE的ViT人脸识别模型可解释性的有效方法。它通过“吸收”注意力伪影来净化注意力图，同时保持或提升模型性能。最终模型ViT-8R（基于ViT-B架构并加入8个寄存器标记）不仅在主流基准上性能领先，还提供了前所未有的清晰注意力可视化，增强了模型的可信度与可分析性。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision Transformers (ViTs) for face recognition (FR) have moved beyond the standard CLS-token paradigm. In this paradigm, a special classification token (CLS) is prepended to the patch embeddings and used as a representation of the input for downstream tasks. An alternative approach, Concatenated Patch Embeddings (CPE), instead leverages all patch tokens by concatenating them into a single vector, which is then projected into a compact face representation. CPE has been shown to improve recognition performance in comparison to CLS-based ones, but our qualitative analysis of attention maps showed the presence of artifacts that limit their interpretability. To address this issue, we incorporate register tokens, learnable tokens concatenated to the initial patch embeddings, and processed jointly through the ViT encoder blocks. This mechanism has been shown to produce more structured and interpretable attention maps compared to baseline ViT. We empirically demonstrate that these artifacts consistently appear across various ViT backbones, including small and large models, and that introducing register tokens effectively mitigates them. Adding four or eight registers significantly enhances interpretability, with eight registers providing the highest verification accuracies and smoothest attention structures. Our resulting model, ViT-8R, corresponds to a CPE-based ViT-B architecture augmented with eight register tokens achieves state-of-the-art performance among ViT-based FR models on large-scale IJB-B and IJB-C benchmarks. Also, ViT-8R produces substantially clearer attention maps compared with the baseline model, which offer deeper insight into the model's attention behavior (https://github.com/TaharChettaoui/ViT-FR-Registers)

</details>


### [73] [Metadata-Aware Multi-Prompt Reasoning for Zero-Shot Accident Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12047)
*Tarandeep Singh,Soumyanetra Pal,Soham Biswas,Nishanth Chandran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: In this paper, we address the problem of zero-shot understanding of accidents from surveillance videos by identifying when an impact event occurs, what type of impact it is, and where in the frame it occurs using natural language. We propose a three-stage pipeline that decomposes the accident understanding into when, what, and where. The first stage extracts a short temporal window around the impact using vision-language similarity. In the second stage, we perform metadata-driven multi-prompt reasoning with five complementary views (baseline, motion, geometry, contrast, and tiebreaker) and resolve disagreement via an entropy-gated pairwise adjudicator. Finally, we localize the impact of an open-vocabulary detector queried on the predicted accident type and scene layout, and aggregate detections across keyframes using a score-weighted centroid. Our pipeline achieves a substantial improvement in the harmonic-mean score over a centre-of-frame baseline on the zero-shot ACCIDENT @ CVPR benchmark. We show that decomposing zero-shot video understanding into temporal localization, semantic classification, and spatial grounding enable more reliable reasoning with vision-language models than direct prompting alone.

</details>


### [74] [MFEN:Multi-Frequency Expert Network for Visible-Infrared Person Re-ID](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12051)
*Xulin Li,Yan Lu,Bin Liu,Qinhong Yang,Qi Chu,Tao Gong,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对可见光-红外人员重识别中模态差异大的问题，本文提出了一种多频率专家网络（MFEN）及其配套训练方法，通过全面的频域学习来提取身份相关特征，提升跨模态重识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见光-红外人员重识别（VI-ReID）因可见光与红外图像间存在显著模态差异而极具挑战。这种差异主要源于不同的光照条件（如光波长和光源类型）。现有基于频率的方法虽能有效提取身份相关轮廓并抑制光照/颜色干扰，但要么未区分不同频带，要么仅关注单一频带，难以应对多样化的光照条件。

Method: 本文提出了一个包含三个互补组件的框架：1）多频率专家网络（MFEN）：采用混合专家设计，进行多频率调制并自适应组合不同频带；2）随机频率增强（RFA）：用于数据增强；3）频率辅助优化（FAO）：用于优化训练过程。三者协同工作，以全面捕获频域关键细节。

Result: 在三个VI-ReID基准数据集上进行了大量实验，结果表明所提方法在跨模态人员重识别任务上具有有效性，能够学习到更稳健的跨模态表示。

Conclusion: 通过综合运用多频率专家网络及配套的增强与优化策略，本文方法能够全面地进行频域学习，有效应对可见光-红外重识别中的模态差异挑战，从而获得更优的性能。

Abstract: Visible-infrared person re-identification (VI-ReID) is challenging due to the large modality discrepancy between visible and infrared images. We contend that this discrepancy is largely related to differing lighting conditions, including differences in light wavelength and light source type. Recently, frequency-based VI-ReID approaches have achieved notable success because frequency information can better extract identity-relevant contours and details while excluding irrelevant lighting and color. However, existing methods either do not distinguish different frequency bands or focus on only one band, which is insufficient under diverse lighting conditions. To perform comprehensive frequency domain learning, we propose a Multi-Frequency Expert Network (MFEN) that enables multi-frequency modulation and adaptively combines different bands through a mixture-of-experts design. We further introduce Random Frequency Augmentation (RFA) and Frequency Auxiliary Optimization (FAO) to better train MFEN. The three modules are complementary and jointly capture critical frequency-domain details for robust representation learning. Extensive experiments on three VI-ReID datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [75] [Performance Analysis of YOLOv11 and YOLOv8 for Mixed Traffic Object Detection under Adverse Weather Conditions in Developing Countries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12066)
*Quoc Thuan Nguyen,Ha Anh Vu,Ngo Dang Thanh Ngan,Minh Phuc Hoang Ngoc*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究对最新的YOLOv11 Nano目标检测架构在复杂驾驶场景下的性能进行了全面评估，并与广泛采用的YOLOv8 Nano基线模型进行了比较。研究发现，YOLOv11n在检测精度、推理速度和计算效率方面提供了更优的权衡，尤其适用于安全关键的边缘部署场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代自动驾驶系统中，车辆需要在恶劣条件（如密集混合交通、雨天和低光照）下保持稳健性能，这是一个关键挑战。因此，有必要对最新的目标检测模型在这些高熵、复杂场景下的表现进行系统评估，以确定其是否适用于实际部署。

Method: 研究采用了一个自定义的融合数据集，结合了印度驾驶数据集（IDD）和伯克利深度驾驶数据集（BDD100K）。评估指标包括检测精度（mean Average Precision, mAP@50）、推理速度（FPS）和计算效率（FLOPs）。分析重点在于YOLOv11 Nano与YOLOv8 Nano基线模型之间的性能权衡。

Result: YOLOv11n在mAP@50上达到了46.6%，其精度（Precision）相比基线模型提高了3.2%，有效降低了复杂场景中的误报率。在计算效率方面，该模型所需的浮点运算次数（FLOPs）减少了22%（6.3G 对比 8.1G），同时在Tesla T4 GPU上保持了70.9 FPS的实时推理速度。

Conclusion: YOLOv11 Nano架构在应对复杂驾驶场景时，在检测精度、推理速度和计算效率方面表现出更佳的性能平衡。其减少的计算负载和保持的实时性，使其成为安全关键边缘部署应用中的一个有前景的优化选择。

Abstract: In modern vehicular systems, robust performance under harsh conditions has become a critical problem of autonomous driving. Our study delivers a comprehensive evaluation of the newest iteration of the YOLO series, which is YOLOv11 Nano architecture benchmarked against the widely adopted YOLOv8 Nano as a baseline on a custom fused dataset that combines the Indian Driving Dataset (IDD) [1] and Berkeley Deep Drive Dataset (BDD100K) [2]. We have analyzed the trade-offs among detection accuracy, inference speed, and computational efficiency in high-entropy scenarios involving dense mixed traffic, rain, and low-light conditions. Specifically, YOLOv11n achieves a mean Average Precision (mAP@50) of 46.6%, with a notable 3.2% improvement in Precision over the baseline, effectively reducing false positives in cluttered scenes. Furthermore, the proposed model exhibits enhanced energy efficiency, requiring 22% fewer FLOPs (6.3G vs. 8.1G) while maintaining real-time inference speed of 70.9 FPS on a Tesla T4 GPU, offering an optimal trade-off for safety-critical edge deployment.

</details>


### [76] [Tac-DINO: Learning Vision-Tactile Features with Patch Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12069)
*Hong Li,Yankang Dong,Yue Xu,Yihan Tang,Mingzhu Li,Jiamin Qiu,Qihang Yao,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Nan Xue,Yong-Lu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究针对触觉学习中视觉与触觉局部-全局对齐不足及数据缺乏的问题，构建了大规模触觉数据集，提出了视觉-触觉全息匹配基准和视觉-触觉块对齐（VTPA）表征学习方法，实验结果表明其优于无对齐方法并实现了与全物体图像的有效对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前触觉学习主要集中在图像级预训练或对齐，但触觉信号本质上对应物体局部接触区域，而关于尺度对齐和全息匹配的研究仍然有限，且缺乏合适的数据集和评估基准。这阻碍了视觉与触觉信息在局部细节和整体语义层面的有效融合。

Method: 1. 构建数据收集系统，获取包含505个真实物体超过20,000次触觉接触的大规模触觉数据集。2. 基于此数据集，设计视觉-触觉全息匹配基准（Vis-Tac Holographic Matching Benchmark），用于评估视觉-触觉局部到全局的对齐能力。3. 提出视觉-触觉块对齐（VTPA）方法，用于视觉-触觉表征学习。

Result: 实验证明，所提出的VTPA方法在视觉-触觉表征学习任务上，性能超越了不使用对齐策略的基线方法，并且能够实现与全物体图像的良好对齐。

Conclusion: 通过构建专用数据集、设计评估基准并提出有效的对齐学习方法，本研究成功弥合了视觉与触觉在局部接触和整体语义之间的表征差距，为多模态触觉感知与理解提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Touch is the primary medium through which humans interact with the environment. Currently, tactile learning mainly focuses on image-level pretraining or alignment. However, tactile signals correspond to local object contact, while research into scale alignment and holographic matching remains limited and proper datasets and benchmarks also lack. To bridge this gap, we first construct a data collection system to acquire a large-scale tactile dataset, with over 20 K tactile contacts from 505 real-world objects. Building on this dataset, we design a Vis-Tac Holographic Matching Benchmark to evaluate vision-tactile local-to-global alignment ability. Then we propose Vision-Tactile Patch Alignment (VTPA) methods for vision-tactile representation learning. Experiments demonstrate that these exceed the performance of methods without alignment and align with whole-object images.

</details>


### [77] [World Model Self-Distillation: Training World Models to Solve General Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12072)
*Sebastian Stapf,Pablo Acuaviva Huertos,Aram Davtyan,Paolo Favaro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过结合自蒸馏和强化学习，提升预训练视频生成器任务解决能力的可扩展框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的视频生成器作为有潜力的视觉世界模型，展现出涌现的任务解决能力；但它们依赖详细的文本描述，限制了其在规划与决策中的直接应用。现有方法要么将推理外包给语言或视觉-语言模型，要么依赖于昂贵且难以扩展的监督微调。

Method: 提出一个结合自蒸馏与强化学习的可扩展框架。给定一个未标注的场景图像，一个视觉-语言模型生成候选任务和详细的逐步解决方案。该解决方案作为条件输入到一个预训练的视频扩散模型（演示器）；然后，将演示器的行为蒸馏到一个仅以图像和简短任务提示为条件的执行器中。这实现了将“基于字幕的生成”的执行知识迁移到“基于指令的任务解决”，无需策划任务-视频监督数据。最后，利用视觉-语言模型的反馈对执行器进行强化学习改进，利用了“判断采样视频是否满足任务”与“生成解决方案”之间的非对称性。

Result: 在本文提出的WorldTasks-Benchmark和DreamGen机器人基准测试上的实验表明，在所提出的基于视觉-语言模型的评估协议下，执行器超越了演示器，并在机器人任务迁移中表现出竞争力。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效激发预训练视频生成模型的任务解决能力，通过自蒸馏和强化学习的结合，提供了一种可扩展且无需昂贵监督数据的解决方案，并在基准测试中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Pretrained video generators are promising visual world models that exhibit emergent task-solving abilities; however, their reliance on detailed textual descriptions limits their direct use for planning and decision-making. Existing approaches either outsource this reasoning to language or vision-language models, or rely on supervised fine-tuning with paired task-execution videos, which are costly to collect and difficult to scale. We propose a scalable framework that elicits task-solving ability in such models by combining self-distillation with reinforcement learning. Given an unlabeled scene image, a vision-language model generates a candidate task and a detailed step-by-step solution. The solution conditions a pretrained video diffusion model, the Demonstrator; we distill its behavior into an Executor conditioned only on the image and a short task prompt. This transfers execution knowledge from caption-guided generation to instruction-conditioned task solving without curated task-video supervision. We further improve the Executor with reinforcement learning from VLM feedback, exploiting the asymmetry between judging whether a sampled video satisfies a task and generating the solution. Experiments on our proposed WorldTasks-Benchmark and the DreamGen robotics benchmark show that the Executor surpasses the Demonstrator under our VLM-based evaluation protocol and transfers competitively to robotic tasks.

</details>


### [78] [Non-frontal face recognition using GANs and memristor-based classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12074)
*Semih Vazgecen,Cristian Sestito,Spyros Stathopoulos,Themis Prodromakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级人脸识别框架，通过集成基于生成对抗网络的姿态正面化与基于忆阻器的神经形态识别技术，解决了无人机等资源受限平台上非正面人脸图像的识别难题，在两个数据集上达到了高达96%的识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习人脸识别系统计算开销大，难以在无人机等资源受限平台进行本地部署，同时面临非正面面部图像的挑战；忆阻器神经形态系统为边缘AI应用提供了一种高效、可扩展的计算范式。

Method: 集成轻量级基于生成对抗网络的非正面姿态正面化方法，与基于忆阻器的神经形态识别模块，形成一个端到端的高效人脸识别框架。

Result: 在两个数据集上的实验表明，结合对抗学习与忆阻器技术的方案有效，识别准确率最高可达96%。

Conclusion: 所提方法缓解了传统AI的计算瓶颈，为人脸识别在动态真实环境中的部署提供了一种可扩展、高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Face recognition systems have advanced significantly through deep learning techniques, delivering high performance and robustness in complex scenarios. However, these approaches incur substantial computational overhead, limiting their in situ applicability in resource-constrained platforms such as drones, where they can address challenges including non-frontal facial imagery. Memristor-based neuromorphic systems have emerged as a compelling approach for edge AI applications, combining biologically inspired processing with efficient and scalable computation. In this work, we propose a facial recognition framework that addresses non-frontal pose variations by integrating lightweight generative adversarial network (GAN)-based pose frontalisation with memristor-based neuromorphic recognition. The experimental results on two datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of combining adversarial learning with memristive technology, achieving up to 96% identification accuracy. The proposed approach alleviates the computational bottlenecks of conventional AI and offers a scalable, efficient solution for face recognition in dynamic real-world environments.

</details>


### [79] [ISAP-3D: Identity-Slot Aligned Part-Aware 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12099)
*Junlin Hao,Haoshuai Fu,Xibin Song,Wei Li,Ruigang Yang,Xinggong Zhang,Jinchuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对现有部分感知3D生成方法因身份-槽排列自由度导致的结构模糊问题，本文提出ISAP-3D框架，通过语义身份令牌和一对一布局预测实现身份对齐，并构建了相应数据集，在结构稳定性与可控性上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部分感知3D生成方法在合成具有语义部件的结构化对象时，常因部件身份与空间布局的隐式耦合或对强布局条件的依赖，而出现部件分配不稳定（如槽位交换、部件合并）的结构模糊性。作者将问题根源归结为“身份-槽排列自由度”：由于缺乏显式的身份-槽对齐，训练过程中语义部件与生成槽之间的对应关系不可辨识，导致同一监督信号可对应多种槽位分配，引发分解不一致。因此，实现稳定生成的关键在于建立基于身份对齐的一对一槽位建模。

Method: 提出身份-槽对齐框架ISAP-3D：1) 使用语义身份令牌（semantic identity tokens）锚定每个部件；2) 在身份条件下进行一对一的布局预测；3) 在布局条件下合成几何结构。该框架通过结构化的局部-全局条件（structured local-global conditioning），在语义、空间和几何各阶段保持身份对齐。同时，构建了一个带有统一语义协议的部分级数据集，以支持可学习且一致的身份-槽对齐。

Result: 广泛的实验证明，所提方法在结构稳定性、可控性及鲁棒性方面均优于当前先进的部分感知生成基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式的身份-槽对齐机制，ISAP-3D有效解决了部分感知3D生成中的结构模糊性问题，为生成结构化、语义清晰的3D对象提供了可靠框架。

Abstract: Part-aware 3D generation aims to synthesize structured objects with semantically meaningful components, yet often suffers from structural ambiguity due to identity-layout entanglement. Existing methods either infer part identity and spatial layout implicitly, which can lead to unstable part allocation (e.g., slot swapping or part merging), or rely on strong layout conditions that are difficult to obtain in practice. We attribute this ambiguity to identity-slot permutation freedom: without explicit identity-slot alignment, the correspondence between semantic parts and generation slots is not identifiable during training, allowing multiple slot assignments to fit the same supervision and leading to inconsistent decomposition. Based on this insight, we argue that stable part-aware generation requires identity-aligned one-to-one slot modelling. We therefore propose an identity-slot aligned framework, ISAP-3D, which anchors each part with semantic identity tokens and performs identity-conditioned one-to-one layout prediction, followed by layout-conditioned geometry synthesis. Structured local-global conditioning maintains identity alignment across semantic, spatial, and geometric stages. We also construct a part-level dataset with a unified semantic protocol to enable learnable and consistent identity-slot alignment. Extensive experiments demonstrate improved structural stability, controllability, and robustness over state-of-the-art part-aware generation baselines.

</details>


### [80] [MSUE: Multi-Modal Soccer Understanding Expert](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12106)
*Litao Li,Yibo Yu,Yufeng Hu,Zhuo Yang,Jiali Wen,Yixin Chen,Yixi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了在2026年SoccerNet VQA挑战赛中获得第三名的解决方案，该方案包含两个核心组件：一个由视觉语言模型驱动的数据合成管线，以及一个名为MSUE的多专家问答架构。该架构通过大语言模型动态调度文本、图像和视频专家（分别为Gemini3-Flash、微调的Qwen3-VL和外部知识库），最终在挑战赛基准测试中达到了0.95的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决2026年SoccerNet视觉问答（VQA）挑战赛中的问题。其动机在于提升视觉问答模型在复杂体育场景（如足球比赛视频）中的性能，并探索如何利用大型模型高效构建训练数据以及设计协作式的多专家系统。

Method: 1. **数据合成管线**：开发了一种由视觉语言模型驱动的、成本效益高的数据合成流程，能够将原始领域数据系统性地重构为多样化的VQA样本，涵盖简短答案和长文本回应。  
2. **多专家问答架构（MSUE）**：提出了一种名为MSUE的架构，其核心是一个大型语言模型，负责将问题动态分发给不同的专家模块。专家包括：文本专家（强大的基线模型Gemini3-Flash）、图像专家（针对任务微调的Qwen3-VL模型）和视频/知识专家（一个外部知识库）。这些专家协同工作以增强视觉问答性能。

Result: 所提出的MSUE架构在SoccerNet VQA挑战赛的官方基准测试中取得了**0.95**的准确率，凭借该成绩在最终排行榜上获得了**第三名**。

Conclusion: 本文提出的由VLM驱动的数据合成管线和多专家协同问答架构MSUE是有效的。该方案展示了利用先进视觉语言模型和大语言模型来构建高质量、多样化VQA数据集，以及通过动态多专家调度机制整合不同模态（文本、图像、视频）和外部知识，是提升复杂领域视觉问答任务性能的一条可行路径。

Abstract: This paper presents our solution to the 2026 SoccerNet VQA Challenge. We first develop a cost-effective data synthesis pipeline driven by a Vision-Language Model (VLM), which systematically restructures raw domain data into diverse VQA samples, including concise answers and long-form responses. Second, we propose MSUE, a multi-expert question answering architecture that employs a Large Language Model (LLM) to dynamically dispatch questions to text, image, and video experts. These experts are instantiated as a strong text baseline Gemini3-Flash, a fine-tuned Qwen3-VL, and an external knowledge base, respectively, working collaboratively to enhance VQA performance. MSUE achieves an accuracy of \textbf{0.95} on the challenge benchmark, securing third place in the leaderboard.

</details>


### [81] [Q-Fold: Query-Aware Focus-Context Spatio-Temporal Folding for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12125)
*Biao Tang,Xu Chen,Shuxiang Gou,Jingyi Yuan,Yuhan Zhang,Chenqiang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Q-Fold 是一种无需训练的长视频理解输入构建框架，通过在查询引导下对连续时序片段构建异质的“焦点-上下文”表示，在有限视觉预算内同时保留关键视觉证据与广泛时序覆盖，显著提升多模态大模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频包含数千帧，处理成本高。现有方法虽构建紧凑视觉输入，但多采用以帧为中心的范式，对保留内容使用相似表示，难以同时维持高保真度的视觉细节和广泛的时间上下文，导致长视频理解效果受限。

Method: 提出 Q-Fold 框架。其核心是以连续时序片段（而非孤立帧）为基本单元。在查询（问题或任务）指导下，将片段分为两类：与查询相关的片段保留为高保真的“焦点帧”；相关性较低的片段则被“折叠”成保持时序顺序的上下文布局。从而构建异质的“焦点-上下文”混合输入。

Result: 在四个长视频基准测试上，使用多种 Video-MLLM（视频多模态大语言模型）进行实验。Q-Fold 在未增加输入视觉预算的情况下，持续提升模型性能。在其中一个超长视频基准上，性能提升最高达 9.1 个百分点。

Conclusion: Q-Fold 通过基于查询引导的异质表示构建策略，有效解决了长视频理解中高保真度证据与广泛时序覆盖难以兼顾的挑战，为多模态大模型提供了无需额外训练或增加计算成本的高效输入方案。

Abstract: Long-video understanding remains challenging for multimodal large language models, because temporally extended videos often contain thousands of frames and are therefore expensive to process exhaustively. Existing methods usually construct compact visual inputs from long videos under a limited visual budget. However, most of them still follow a frame-centric paradigm and apply similar representations to retained content regardless of its importance. This makes it difficult to preserve both high-fidelity visual evidence and broad temporal coverage. To address this issue, we propose Q-Fold, a training-free input construction framework for long-video understanding. Instead of treating isolated frames as the basic modeling unit, Q-Fold operates on contiguous temporal segments and constructs a heterogeneous Focus--Context representation under query guidance. Query-relevant segments are preserved as high-fidelity Focus Frames, while less relevant segments are folded into chronology-preserving contextual layouts. In this way, Q-Fold preserves critical visual evidence and broad temporal coverage, while better maintaining local temporal continuity within short segments. Experiments on four long-video benchmarks with multiple Video-MLLMs show that Q-Fold consistently improves performance without increasing the input budget. Notably, it achieves gains of up to 9.1 percentage points on an ultra-long video benchmark. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [82] [AGE-MIL: Anchor-Guided Evidence Learning for Patient-Level Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12126)
*Jiawei Niu,Jian Chen,Di Zhang,Junbo Lu,Zhangcheng Liao,Xuhao Liu,Honglin Zhong,Mireia Crispin-Ortuzar,Chen Li,Zeyu Gao,Yi Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AGE-MIL是一个用于患者级别预测的弱监督框架，通过构建患者级别锚点来指导局部斑块的检索与整合，解决了传统多实例学习方法在整合多全切片图像证据时的不稳定性问题，并在实验中持续优于八种现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算病理学方法主要在单个全切片图像级别采用多实例学习范式，而病理学家在实际诊断中需要整合来自多个全切片图像的证据进行患者级别诊断。这种范式与实践的不匹配，导致直接将患者级别的监督信号应用于传统MIL框架时，常常引起优化过程不稳定和预测可靠性下降。

Method: 提出一种名为“锚点引导证据多实例学习”的弱监督框架。该方法首先从多个全切片图像的表征中构建一个患者级别的锚点，以捕获全局病理学上下文。然后，利用该锚点指导检索并整合与诊断相关的局部图像斑块，从而实现稳健的患者级别建模。患者级别的风险进一步被建模为一个证据积累过程，这有助于在弱监督条件下促进优化过程的稳定性。

Result: 在来自两个独立队列的六个临床相关患者级别预测任务上进行了评估。实验结果表明，所提出的AGE-MIL框架在所有任务上均持续优于八种最先进的多实例学习方法，证明了其有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: AGE-MIL通过其锚点引导和证据积累的机制，有效解决了将传统MIL应用于患者级别预测时面临的优化不稳定问题，实现了对多个全切片图像证据的稳健整合与建模，为计算病理学中的患者级别分析提供了一种更可靠的弱监督解决方案。

Abstract: Existing computational pathology methods predominantly operate within whole-slide image (WSI)-level multiple instance learning (MIL) paradigms, while patient-level modeling remains underexplored. In routine pathological practice, however, pathologists derive diagnostic and prognostic conclusions by integrating evidence across multiple WSIs rather than relying on any single slide. This discrepancy creates a fundamental misalignment when patient-level supervision is directly imposed on conventional MIL frameworks, often leading to unstable optimization and degraded predictive reliability. To address this issue, we propose Anchor-Guided Evidence MIL (AGE-MIL), a weakly supervised framework for patient-level prediction. AGE-MIL constructs a patient-level anchor from slide representations to capture global pathological context and guide the retrieval and integration of diagnostically relevant local patches, enabling robust patient-level modeling. Patient-level risk is further modeled as an evidence accumulation process, promoting stable optimization under weak supervision. AGE-MIL is evaluated on six clinically relevant patient-level prediction tasks from two independent cohorts. Experimental results show that the proposed framework consistently outperforms eight state-of-the-art MIL methods. Code is available at https://github.com/wodeniua/AGE-MIL.

</details>


### [83] [Time-Conditioned and Multi-Time Survival Prediction from 2D PET/CT Projections in Lung Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12140)
*Ashish Chauhan,Sambit Tarai,Elin Lundström,Johan Öfverstedt,Håkan Ahlström,Joel Kullberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Accurate prediction of overall survival (OS) from positron emission tomography/computed tomography (PET/CT) can support personalized treatment and follow-up strategies in oncology. However, the impact of temporal modeling on imaging-based survival prediction remains insufficiently explored. We investigate how different temporal formulations influence survival prediction by developing two complementary approaches: Attention-guided Time-Conditioned Survival (ATCS) and Multi-Time Survival (MTS). We retrospectively analyzed pre-treatment PET/CT images from 848 patients with non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC), including 556 for model development and 292 for held-out testing. A previously proposed Time-Conditioned Survival (TCS) model was used as a baseline. Models were trained using 5-fold cross-validation and evaluated on the test set using time-dependent area under the curve (AUC) at 6-month intervals from 0.5 to 5 years. Both ATCS and MTS outperformed the baseline TCS model, achieving mean AUCs of 0.794 and 0.793, respectively, compared to 0.767. ATCS performed better at earlier time points (0.5-3 years), whereas MTS performed better at later intervals (3.5-5 years). Combining tumor-specific and tissue-wise PET/CT features improved performance over either input alone. Finer temporal discretization improved short-term prediction, while coarser intervals provided more stable long-term estimates. These findings demonstrate that temporal modeling and input design influence PET/CT-based survival prediction. The proposed approaches enable time-specific survival estimation from pre-treatment imaging and may support improved risk stratification and clinical decision-making.

</details>


### [84] [TopoCap: Learning Topology-Agnostic Motion Priors for Monocular Video-to-Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12153)
*Cheng-Feng Pu,Jia-Peng Zhang,Meng-Hao Guo,Yan-Pei Cao,Shi-Min Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TopoCap是首个统一框架，能从单目视频中提取动作并无缝重定向至具有任意、未见过的骨骼拓扑（如双足、六足乃至无生命物体）的角色，无需测试时优化。其核心是利用图CVAE学习一个“通用运动流形”，将运动动力学与骨骼拓扑解耦，并通过条件流匹配从视觉特征预测拓扑无关的运动编码。为此，作者构建了包含5000余种拓扑、200万帧的Mobjaverse大规模数据集。实验表明，TopoCap在人类和四足基准上超越了专用模型，并能实现零样本重定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式3D资产的激增带来了巨大的动画需求，但现有的动作捕捉方法仍然脆弱且受限。它们要么依赖于特定物种的模板（如SMPL），要么需要费时费力的手动绑定，无法处理具有任意骨骼拓扑的广泛3D角色。因此，亟需一种能泛化到任意结构、自动化程度高的统一运动提取与重定向方法。

Method: 方法包含两个阶段，核心是将运动动力学从离散、组合式的骨骼拓扑中解耦：1. **学习通用运动流形**：使用一个图条件变分自编码器，将来自异构运动链的运动压缩到一个共享的、固定长度的潜在空间中。解码器以目标骨骼的“结构嵌入”为条件，从而将运动与拓扑分离。2. **视频到动画的条件生成**：将从视频到动画的任务建模为一个“条件流匹配”问题。模型从视频特征预测出上述拓扑无关的运动潜在码，从而实现动作的提取与迁移。为训练此模型，作者构建了Mobjaverse数据集，从Objaverse-XL中策划，包含超过5000种独特的骨骼拓扑和200万帧动画。

Result: 在标准的人体（如Human3.6M）和四足动物动作重定向基准上，TopoCap的表现优于现有的专用模型。更重要的是，它成功展示了对长尾分布的3D生物（即数据集中未见过的、拓扑结构复杂的角色）进行零样本动作重定向的能力。同时，作者开源了其大规模、高多样性的Mobjaverse数据集。

Conclusion: TopoCap提供了一个开创性的统一框架，解决了从单目视频到任意骨骼拓扑3D角色的自动化动作重定向这一难题。它通过学习一个与拓扑无关的通用运动流形，实现了卓越的泛化能力，超越了传统依赖于特定模板的方法。同时，其构建的大规模数据集为相关研究提供了宝贵的资源，推动了该领域的发展。

Abstract: The explosion of generative 3D assets has created a massive demand for animation, yet current motion capture methods remain brittle, restricted to species-specific templates (e.g., SMPL) or requiring labor-intensive manual rigging. We introduce TopoCap, the first unified framework capable of extracting motion from monocular video and retargeting it onto characters with arbitrary, unseen skeletal topologies, i.e., from bipeds to hexapods and inanimate objects, without test-time optimization. Our key insight is that while skeletal structures are combinatorial and discrete, the underlying physics of motion occupy a continuous, low-dimensional manifold. We materialize this insight via a two-stage generative pipeline. First, we learn a Universal Motion Manifold using a Graph CVAE that compresses heterogeneous kinematic chains into a shared, fixed-length latent code. By explicitly conditioning the decoder on a structural embedding of the target rig, we disentangle motion dynamics from skeletal topology. Second, we treat video-to-animation as a conditional flow matching problem, predicting these topology-agnostic codes from visual features. To learn this generalized prior, we introduce Mobjaverse, a massive-scale dataset curated from Objaverse-XL. Comprising over 5,000 unique skeletal topologies and 2 million frames, it exceeds the structural diversity of existing datasets by two orders of magnitude. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \MethodMotion outperforms specialist models on human and quadruped benchmarks while enabling zero-shot retargeting for the long tail of 3D creatures. Dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/duckduckplz/Mobjaverse.

</details>


### [85] [OpenMedReason: Scientific Reasoning Supervision for Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12169)
*Negin Baghbanzadeh,Pritam Sarkar,Michael Colacci,Abeer Badawi,Adibvafa Fallahpour,Arash Afkanpour,Leonid Sigal,Ali Etemad,Elham Dolatabadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了OpenMedReason医学推理数据集及配套评估基准，通过人类撰写的科学文章构建高质量推理轨迹，显著提升了大型视觉语言模型在医学领域的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在临床等高风险场景中，大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）需要基于视觉证据和临床知识的推理过程，而不仅仅是得出正确答案。现有方法可能依赖合成的思维链，缺乏真实、可溯源的推理监督。

Method: 1. 构建OpenMedReason数据集：一个约45万实例的大规模、开放多模态医学推理语料库，其推理轨迹主要源自人工整理的生物医学科学文章，覆盖多种医学视觉模态。
2. 创建OpenMedReason-Bench基准：用于从感知、医学知识和逻辑推理三个维度对LVLMs进行细粒度评估的基准。
3. 训练与评估：使用该数据集进行监督微调（SFT）和基于强化学习的对齐训练，并评估模型性能。

Result: 1. 准确率提升：使用OpenMedReason训练后，模型在视觉问答（VQA）上的准确率平均比基线模型提升了20%。
2. 性能接近顶尖：性能与可比规模的最强医学LVLMs仅相差4.2%。
3. 全面能力提升：细粒度分析证实，模型在感知、医学知识和逻辑推理三个维度上均有提升，并非单一维度的改善。
4. 推理质量获认可：在86.1%的成对比较中，人类评估者认为OpenMedReason训练的模型产生的推理轨迹优于基线模型。

Conclusion: OpenMedReason作为一个基于权威文献的高质量医学推理数据集，能有效且全面地提升大型视觉语言模型的医学推理能力。其配套的评估基准为超越最终答案准确率的细粒度模型评估提供了有效工具。

Abstract: High-stakes clinical use of large vision-language models (LVLMs) requires reasoning that is grounded in visual evidence and clinical knowledge, not just correct final answers. We introduce OpenMedReason, a large-scale, open multimodal medical reasoning corpus comprising approximately 450K image-question-answer instances whose reasoning traces are primarily derived from curated biomedical, human-authored scientific articles. OpenMedReason provides high-fidelity supervision beyond synthetic chains of thought, covering diverse medical domain vision modalities such as radiological scans, microscopic images, visible light photographs, charts, and others. We complement it with OpenMedReason-Bench, a held-out benchmark that allows fine-grained evaluation of LVLMs along three complementary axes of capability, including perception, medical knowledge, and rationale, enabling diagnostic evaluation beyond final-answer accuracy. OpenMedReason is a rich training resource that exhibits its effectiveness in both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement-based alignment. Training with OpenMedReason yields a 20% average improvement in VQA accuracy over the base model and achieves performance within 4.2% of the strongest comparable-scale medical LVLMs. Fine-grained performance analysis confirms that the gains are not concentrated in any single axis: OpenMedReason improves perception, medical knowledge, and rationale jointly, and its reasoning traces are preferred over those of the base model in 86.1% of pairwise comparisons. We release the code and dataset at huggingface.co/datasets/neginb/OpenMedReason.

</details>


### [86] [Beyond Dark Knowledge: Mixup-Based Distillation for Reliable Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12171)
*José Medina,Paul Honeine,Abdelaziz Bensrhair,Amnir Hadachi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了知识蒸馏（KD）与mixup技术结合时的一种特定情境：仅在学生训练阶段应用mixup。研究发现，这种设置导致教师模型在未见过的vicinal分布输入上产生监督信号，其主导是分布混淆而非类别间结构。然而，学生模型超越了单纯模仿，独立获得了vicinal区域更高的线性度，从而构建了一个更丰富的知识传输通道。该方法能持续提升学生准确率并显著降低其过度自信，同时校准特性可从教师独立于准确率地传递给学生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏（KD）和mixup均能有效诱导类别边界的平滑性：KD通过概率分布捕获固有的类别关系，而mixup则通过输入的凸组合强制实现平滑。然而，当mixup仅在学生训练阶段应用时，两者的交互作用机制尚不清楚。在此设置下，教师模型需要评估其从未在训练中见过的vicinal分布输入，这种“控制性不匹配”对知识转移的影响尚未被表征。本文旨在探究这种不匹配如何影响知识转移，以及学生模型如何在这种约束下学习。

Method: 本文在CIFAR和ImageNet数据集上，使用不同容量的教师模型进行实验。核心方法是将mixup仅应用于学生训练阶段，从而在教师与学生的训练数据分布之间引入“控制性不匹配”。通过分析教师模型在vicinal分布输入上的响应（监督信号），以及学生模型在该设置下的学习行为，来表征这种不匹配的影响。此外，研究了温度缩放（temperature scaling）在vicinal训练下对准确率-校准权衡的作用。

Result: 研究发现：1）教师模型在vicinal分布输入上的监督信号被“分布混淆”主导，而非有意义的类间结构。2）尽管存在不匹配，学生模型并未单纯模仿教师，而是独立地获得了vicinal区域内更高的线性度，这是一种教师所缺乏的结构属性，表明其学习超越了“暗知识”转移。3）相比基线，结合mixup的KD能持续提高学生准确率，并将过度自信降低一个数量级（在CIFAR和ImageNet上均如此）。4）模型的校准特性可以从教师传播到学生，且这种传播独立于准确率的转移。5）温度缩放控制着一个可测量的准确率-校准权衡，这种权衡在vicinal训练（即使用mixup）下变得更加显著。

Conclusion: 本文将结合mixup的知识蒸馏重新定义为一种更丰富的知识传输通道，而非传统标准KD的退化版本。在仅对学生应用mixup的特定设置下，尽管教师的监督信号存在分布混淆，学生模型能通过独立学习vicinal区域的线性结构，实现判别性能（准确率）、不确定性估计（校准/过度自信）和表示几何（线性度）的同时优化。这揭示了模型间知识迁移在输入分布失配条件下的复杂适应性机制。

Abstract: Knowledge Distillation (KD) and mixup have proven effective at inducing smoothness in class boundaries; KD captures inherent class relationships in probability distributions, and mixup enforces them through convex combinations of inputs. Their interaction, however, remains poorly understood, particularly when mixup is applied only during student training. In this setting, the teacher is queried on inputs drawn from a vicinal distribution it never saw during training, a controlled mismatch whose effect on knowledge transfer has not been characterised. We show that this mismatch causes the teacher's supervisory signal to be dominated by distributional confusion rather than inter-class structure. Despite it, the student does not merely imitate the teacher: it independently acquires greater linearity in the vicinal region, a structural property that the teacher lacks, and goes beyond dark-knowledge transfer. KD with mixup consistently improves student accuracy and reduces overconfidence by an order of magnitude relative to the baseline, across CIFAR and ImageNet with varying-capacity teachers. Crucially, calibration propagates from teacher to student independently of accuracy transfer, and temperature scaling governs a measurable accuracy-calibration trade-off that becomes more pronounced under vicinal training. These results reframe mixup distillation not as a degraded version of standard KD, but as a richer transfer channel that simultaneously shapes discriminative performance, uncertainty estimation, and representational geometry.

</details>


### [87] [DynaTok: Token-Based 4D Reconstruction from Partial Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12189)
*Weirong Chen,Keisuke Tateno,Hidenobu Matsuki,Michael Niemeyer,Daniel Cremers,Federico Tombari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DynaTok是一种用于从部分点云序列进行4D重建的基于点云的框架，无需图像输入或显式对应关系，通过编码器-解码器架构实现完整的、时间一致的4D点云序列重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法大多依赖图像，点云方法通常局限于单个物体、假设输入相对完整或需要显式对应关系，这限制了其在几何观测不完整、无序且缺乏时间对应关系场景中的应用。DynaTok旨在解决这些局限性。

Method: DynaTok将每一帧点云编码为紧凑的潜在标记，使用基于Transformer的时空编码器在时间上聚合不完整观测，并通过残余标记在统一模型中解耦几何与运动。流匹配解码器随后根据这些潜在标记重建出完整的、时间一致的4D点云序列。

Result: 在物体和场景级别的基准测试中，DynaTok从部分点云观测中实现了重建质量的提升和更好的时间连贯性。

Conclusion: DynaTok是一个有效的无图像、无对应关系、基于点云的4D重建框架，能够从不完整的点云序列中恢复完整且时间一致的动态场景，并在实验中证明了其优越性。

Abstract: We address 4D reconstruction from partial point cloud sequences, where depth-sensor observations are incomplete, unordered, and lack explicit temporal correspondences. This geometry-only setting is challenging due to missing observations and ambiguous dynamics. While recent progress has largely relied on image-based methods, existing point-based approaches typically focus on single objects, assume relatively complete inputs, or require explicit correspondences. To address these limitations, we propose DynaTok, a point-based framework for correspondence-free 4D reconstruction from partial point cloud sequences without images. DynaTok encodes frames into compact latent tokens, aggregates incomplete observations over time with a Transformer-based spatiotemporal encoder, and decouples geometry and motion through residual tokens in a unified model. A flow-matching decoder then reconstructs complete, temporally consistent 4D point-cloud sequences conditioned on the latent tokens. Experiments on object- and scene-level benchmarks demonstrate improved reconstruction quality and temporal coherence from partial point cloud observations. Project page: https://wrchen530.github.io/dynatok/.

</details>


### [88] [InternVideo3: Agentify Foundation Models with Multimodal Contextual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12195)
*Ziang Yan,Sheng Xia,Jiashuo Yu,Yue Wu,Tianxiang Jiang,Songze Li,Kanghui Tian,Yicheng Xu,Yinan He,Kai Chen,Limin Wang,Yu Qiao,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了InternVideo3框架，通过多模态上下文推理（MCR）和新型注意力机制提升长时程视频理解与交互能力，并在多个基准测试中取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开源基础模型研究主要聚焦于文本主导的场景，对需要持续时序理解和迭代交互的长时程多模态任务（特别是视频任务）探索不足，存在明显研究空白。

Method: 1. 提出多模态上下文推理（MCR）框架，将理解视为一个基于共享、演进上下文的闭环过程；2. 引入多模态多头潜在注意力（M²LA），通过保留式重新参数化来压缩KV缓存状态；3. 采用分阶段训练：持续预训练、短到长的监督微调、基于规则的强化学习和在线策略蒸馏。

Result: 在Video-MME、MLVU和EgoSchema等基准测试中取得了强劲的性能表现；成功将模型实例化为带有检索工具的视频智能体，展现出基于证据的稳健行为。

Conclusion: 高效的上下文处理和闭环推理机制对于使开源多模态模型适应长时程、基于视觉的智能体任务至关重要。

Abstract: Recent progress in foundation models has shifted toward agentic behavior involving multi-step reasoning and tool use. However, open-source efforts largely focus on text-dominant settings, leaving long-horizon multimodal tasks underexplored. This gap is evident in video tasks requiring sustained temporal understanding and iterative interaction. We present InternVideo3, a framework enhancing these capabilities via Multimodal Contextual Reasoning (MCR). MCR treats understanding as a closed-loop process over a shared, evolving context containing observations, instructions, reasoning, tool actions, and memory. This frames long-video understanding as evidence accumulation and verification. To ensure efficiency, we introduce Multimodal Multi-head Latent Attention (M^2LA), a token-preserving reparameterization compressing KV-cache states while retaining the full token stream. Our staged training includes continued pretraining, short-to-long supervised fine-tuning, rule-based reinforcement learning, and on-policy distillation. Experiments show InternVideo3 achieves strong performance on benchmarks like Video-MME, MLVU, and EgoSchema. We further instantiate the model as a video agent with retrieval tools, demonstrating robust evidence-grounded behavior. Our results suggest that efficient context handling and closed-loop reasoning are vital for adapting open multimodal models toward long-horizon visually grounded agency.

</details>


### [89] [SHERPA: Seam-aware Harmonized ERP Adaptation for Open-Domain 360$^\circ$ Panorama Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12213)
*Jungwoon Kang,Jaehun Kim,Yiwon Yu,Hyungyum Jang,Sanghoon Lee,Jongyoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Panoramic imagery is increasingly used in world-generation, games, and simulation, where users may need not only photorealistic scenes but also stylized and non-photorealistic environments. Large-scale text-to-image diffusion and flow models provide broad style and semantic priors for this goal, but planar image training misaligns them with the wrap-around topology and polar regions of $360^\circ$ panoramas represented in equirectangular projection (ERP). We present SHERPA, a lightweight adaptation framework that combines frequency-selective Circular RoPE, Circular Latent Encoding/Decoding, image-side FFN adapters, and a Dual-Path Training Scheme. Circular RoPE replaces only the seam-sensitive high-frequency horizontal RoPE band with integer-periodic harmonics while preserving the pretrained lower-frequency spectrum. The Paired Panorama Path supervises geometry, while the Unpaired Style Path uses self-supervised yaw consistency for target-free stylized prompts. As a result, SHERPA generates $360^\circ$ panoramas across both photorealistic panorama domains and open-domain stylized prompts.

</details>


### [90] [MLT-Dedup: Efficient Large-Scale Online Video Deduplication via Multi-Level Representations and Spatial-Temporal Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12215)
*David Yuchen Wang,Haoying Li,Hailun Xu,Wei Chee Yew,Zirui Zhu,Sanjay Saha,Hao Hei,Kanchan Sarkar,Kun Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了MLT-Dedup框架，通过多级表示和时空匹配实现高效的大规模视频去重，在保持高精度的同时显著降低了在线重复率并提升了索引容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着在线平台用户生成视频内容的爆炸式增长，近似重复视频大量出现，这不仅降低了用户体验，还增加了存储和带宽成本。现有的去重框架在有限索引预算下难以检索到足够多的高质量候选，并且在效率和精度之间存在权衡。

Method: 提出MLT-Dedup框架，包含多级视频编码器（ML-VE）用于提取帧级和剪辑级嵌入，其中稀疏嵌入用于高效检索，细粒度嵌入用于精确匹配；并引入差异特征增强相似性模块（DiF-SiM）来定位重复时间段并提供相似性证据。

Result: 在真实大规模平台上的实验表明，MLT-Dedup在90%精度下将在线重复率降低了91%，稀疏检索设计使索引容量增加了5倍。

Conclusion: MLT-Dedup有效解决了大规模视频去重中效率与精度的权衡问题，通过多级表示和时空匹配显著提升了去重性能，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: The explosive growth of user-generated video content on online platforms is accompanied by the emergence of numerous near-duplicate videos--videos that are identical or highly similar but differ by partial edits. These duplicates degrade user experience and increase storage and bandwidth costs, making large-scale video deduplication a critical task. Existing video deduplication frameworks face a fundamental challenge in retrieving sufficient high-quality candidates under a limited index budget, as well as trade-offs between efficiency and precision. To address these issues, we propose MLT-Dedup, an efficient large-scale online video deduplication framework with Multi-Level representations and spatial-Temporal matching. Our approach employs a Multi-Level Video Encoder (ML-VE) to extract both fine-grained frame-level and sparse clip-level embeddings: sparse embeddings support efficient candidate retrieval, while fine-grained embeddings are loaded for precise pairwise matching. During matching, we introduce DiF-SiM, a Differential Feature-enhanced Similarity Module capable of locating duplicated temporal segments and providing reliable similarity evidence to support policy-driven deduplication decisions. Extensive experiments on a real-world large-scale platform demonstrate that MLT-Dedup reduces online repetition rates by 91% at 90% precision. Furthermore, our sparse retrieval design achieves a 5x increase in indexing capacity, enabling broader candidate coverage in real-world deployment.

</details>


### [91] [Adapting Prithvi-EO for Fallow Detection for Food-Water Nexus: ViT-Adapter Necks and Parameter-Efficient Backbone tuning of Geospatial Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12218)
*Sk Muhammad Asif,Orhun Aydin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了一个用于检测休耕地的检测管线，该管线结合了参数高效微调方案（LoRA和混合PEFT）与三种颈部设计（伪多尺度、Lite ViT-Adapter、Full ViT-Adapter）。最佳配置（Lite ViT-Adapter配合单阶段检测头）在Diou损失下实现了0.9479的mAP@50，表明中心感知定位对不规则休耕地检测的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解休耕地的空间分布对于优化粮食-水关系至关重要，因为休耕在作物轮作和节水方面发挥作用。USDA作物数据层中的休耕地分类准确性较低。现有地理空间基础模型（GFM）如Prithvi-EO的视觉转换器（ViT）主干在单空间尺度上生成特征，这不适合目标检测头所需的多尺度特征。现有的多尺度金字塔合成方法通过缩放单步幅词元实现，牺牲了空间异质性，而完全主干微调对于GFM来说计算成本过高。

Method: 作者评估了一个结合两种参数高效微调（PEFT）方案：低秩自适应（LoRA）和混合PEFT，以及三种颈部设计：伪多尺度、Lite ViT-Adapter和Full ViT-Adapter的休耕地检测管线。

Result: 最佳配置（Lite ViT-Adapter配合单阶段检测头）在Diou损失下实现了0.9479的mAP@50。无适配器的单阶段检测（在LoRA下）比无适配器的基于锚框的方法提升了6.42%，而最佳配置比无适配器的基线基于锚框的方法提升了25.70%。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，轻量级空间先验融合和选择性主干解冻使Prithvi-EO能够更有效地捕捉局部休耕模式，其表现优于依赖重新塑形的单步幅ViT词元的方法。

Abstract: Understanding spatial distribution of fallow land is important for optimizing the food-water (FW) nexus, given fallowing's role in crop rotation and water conservation. Fallow is a low accuracy class in USDA Cropland Data Layer (CDL). Geospatial foundation model (GFM), Prithvi-EO has shown strong transferability across computer vision tasks. However, its Vision Transformer (ViT) backbone produces features at a single spatial scale that are ill-suited for the multi-scale features required by object detection heads. Existing approaches synthesise multi-scale pyramids through scaling of single stride tokens, sacrificing spatial heterogeneity, and full backbone fine-tuning is computationally prohibitive for GFMs. We evaluate a fallow detection pipeline combining two parameter-efficient fine tuning (PEFT) schemes: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) and a hybrid PEFT, with three neck designs: pseudo multi-scale, Lite ViT-Adapter, and Full ViT-Adapter. Our best configuration, Lite ViT-Adapter with a one-stage head, achieves a mAP@50 of 0.9479 with the Diou loss, suggesting the effectiveness of center-aware localization for irregular fallow field detection. ViT-Adapter free one-stage detection under LoRA improves the adapter-free anchor-based approach by 6.42%, and the best configuration improves baseline adapter-free anchor-based approach by 25.70%. These results demonstrate that lightweight spatial prior fusion and selective backbone unfreezing enable Prithvi-EO to capture local fallow patterns more effectively, outperforming approaches that rely on reshaped single-stride ViT tokens.

</details>


### [92] [An Electric Potential-Augmented Benchmark Dataset for Physics-Guided Image Reconstruction of Electrical Capacitance Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12226)
*Xinqi Zhang,Qiming Ma,Lihui Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对电容层析成像（ECT）深度学习模型忽略电位场物理联系的问题，本文提出了一个电位增强的基准数据集，并展示了其提升模型准确性和鲁棒性的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法将ECT传感器视为黑盒，直接映射电容与介电常数分布，忽略了控制非线性和不适定“软场”效应的电位场这一基本物理联系。

Method: 基于八电极传感器，通过COMSOL-MATLAB流水线生成包含20,000个随机样本的数据集，涵盖四种典型流型。每个样本包含电容向量、介电常数分布图像以及八个激励方式的全视场电位图。同时提供了前向和逆问题的评估协议。

Result: 通过在分布内（IID）和分布外（OOD）场景的全面测试，系统验证了电位图信息的引入能够显著提升ECT建模的准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 显式包含电位场等潜在场信息，显著降低了将物理定律融入ECT建模的难度，为未来基于物理引导的ECT图像重建机器学习研究建立了标准化基础。

Abstract: While deep learning has significantly advanced image reconstruction of Electrical Capacitance Tomography (ECT), most data-driven methods map directly between capacitance and permittivity distribution, treating the sensor as a black box. This overlooks the electric potential field -- the fundamental physical link governing the nonlinear and ill-posed ``soft-field'' effect. To address this, we propose an electric potential-augmented ECT benchmark dataset designed to explicitly integrate latent physics behind ECT into the learning process. Generated via a COMSOL-MATLAB pipeline for an eight-electrode sensor as an example, the dataset comprises 20,000 randomized samples across four typical flow patterns. Crucially, alongside the conventional capacitance vectors and permittivity distributions depicted as images, each sample preserves eight excitation-wise full-field potential maps. Beyond data release, we provide illustrative evaluation protocols for both forward and inverse problems of ECT. Through comprehensive testing on both in-distribution (IID) and out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios, we systematically demonstrate how the inclusion of electric potential maps enhances modeling accuracy and robustness. Fundamentally, the explicit inclusion of latent field information significantly lowers the barrier to integrating physical laws into ECT modeling, thereby establishing a standardized foundation for future physics-guided machine learning of ECT image reconstruction.

</details>


### [93] [Damage-TriageFormer: A Foundation-Model Framework for Typology-Based Building Damage Assessment from Mono-Temporal Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12248)
*Yiming Xiao,Yu-Hsuan Ho,Sanjay Thasma,Junwei Ma,Ali Mostafavi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Damage-TriageFormer模型，一种仅需灾后单张图像即可对建筑损坏进行类型学分类（而非严重程度评级）的方法，旨在支持更有效的应急资源分配。核心贡献包括：用于损坏类型学分类的新基准数据集DamageTriage-Bench，以及结合了ViT骨干网络、高分辨率特征金字塔和两阶段门控分类头的Damage-TriageFormer模型。模型在验证集和测试集上的宏观F1分数分别达到0.624和0.619，尤其在区分未损坏建筑（F1: 0.91）和完全结构性倒塌（F1: 0.84）上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后建筑损坏评估对于优先分配资源和指导恢复工作至关重要。然而，现有的自动化方法存在局限：要么将损坏简化为单一严重程度等级（如无损坏、轻微、主要、完全损坏），要么需要通常难以获取的配对灾前与灾后图像。这限制了在紧急情况下的应用。因此，有必要开发一种仅依赖灾后单张图像、能提供更丰富、更具操作性的损坏信息（如区分屋顶与结构损坏、部分与完全损坏）的评估方法。

Method: 1. 数据构建：提出了新的基准数据集DamageTriage-Bench，基于NOAA应急响应影像，涵盖飓风迈克尔（2018）、飓风海伦（2024）和2025年洛杉矶野火复合事件。数据集定义了五个类型学类别，区分屋顶损坏与结构损坏，并在每类中区分部分与完全程度。
2. 模型架构：提出了Damage-TriageFormer模型，该模型基于DINOv3 ViT-L骨干网络。主要改进包括：引入Simple Feature Pyramid以提升分辨率进行实例池化；设计了一个两阶段门控损坏分类头；并加入了辅助的严重程度回归目标作为辅助任务。

Result: 模型在DamageTriage-Bench的验证集上取得了0.624的宏观F1分数，在保留的分层测试集上取得了0.619的宏观F1分数。模型在具有操作意义的类别上表现最佳：对于“未损坏建筑”类别的F1分数达到0.91，对于“完全结构性倒塌”类别的F1分数达到0.84。然而，由于样本稀少且标签边界本身存在模糊性，“完全屋顶损坏”这一罕见类别的识别仍然是一个挑战。

Conclusion: 研究表明，仅利用灾后单张图像进行建筑损坏评估是可行的，且能够支持具有操作性的建筑损坏类型学分类。这使得在没有灾前参考图像的情况下，能够进行更有针对性的应急响应和资源分配，解决了现有方法的关键局限。尽管在罕见类别上存在挑战，但该模型和数据集为灾后快速评估提供了新的有效工具和基准。

Abstract: Decision-relevant building damage assessment is critical for prioritizing resources and recovery after a disaster, yet most automated methods either flatten damage into a single severity scale (no damage, minor, major, destroyed) or require paired pre- and post-event imagery that is often unavailable for emerging hazards. This paper presents Damage-TriageFormer, a single-image, post-event, footprint-conditioned model that produces a damage typology rather than a severity scale.
