We contribute: (1) DamageTriage-Bench, a new benchmark built from NOAA Emergency Response Imagery across Hurricane Michael (2018), Hurricane Helene (2024), and the 2025 Los Angeles wildfire complex, with five typology classes that distinguish roof damage from structural damage and, within each, partial from total extent; and (2) Damage-TriageFormer, which extends a DINOv3 ViT-L backbone with a Simple Feature Pyramid for higher-resolution instance pooling, a two-stage gated damage head, and an auxiliary severity-regression objective. Our model achieves macro F1 of 0.624 on validation and 0.619 on a held-out stratified test set, performing strongest where operational triage needs it most, with per-class F1 of 0.91 and 0.84 on undamaged buildings and total structural collapse, respectively. While the rare Total Roof Damage class remains difficult due to its limited examples and an inherently ambiguous label boundary, our results show that single-image post-event imagery can support actionable building damage typing, enabling targeted emergency response and resource allocation without a pre-event reference.

</details>


### [94] [Bridging Day and Night: Unsupervised Cross-Domain Re-Identification with Synergistic Prompt and Prototype Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12258)
*Jiyang Xu,Rui Liu,Hang Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于跨域昼夜行人重识别的无监督新框架。该框架创新性地结合了提示学习和基于原型的表示学习，通过一个两阶段训练策略，在无需人工标注的情况下，实现了跨域身份关联。实验表明，在无监督设置下，其性能可比肩先进的全监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域昼夜行人重识别面临昼夜场景间视觉外观差异巨大的挑战。现有的全监督方法严重依赖费力的人工标注，成本高昂且跨域泛化能力有限。因此，研究无需标签的、更具泛化能力的无监督方法至关重要。

Method: 本文提出了一种协同结合提示学习与原型学习的两阶段无监督框架。第一阶段：利用视觉语言模型，通过实例感知的动态偏差适配，生成实例特定的文本提示，并将视觉特征与文本提示在统一的语义空间进行实例级对齐。第二阶段：构建域特定的原型记忆库，并引入两个互补模块：1）域内身份关联模块，以增强单个域内特征的判别力；2）跨域原型匹配模块，以可靠地识别正负原型对，从而建立跨昼夜域的稳健身份对应关系。

Result: 在公开基准数据集上的大量实验验证了该方法的有效性。在完全无监督的设置下，该框架达到了与最先进的全监督方法相当的Rank-1准确率。

Conclusion: 本文成功提出了一种无需人工标注的跨域昼夜行人重识别框架。通过创新性地结合提示学习与原型学习，并采用两阶段渐进式训练策略，该方法有效克服了昼夜域差异，并在无监督条件下实现了卓越的性能，为跨域ReID研究提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Cross-domain day-night re-identification (ReID) is fundamentally challenged by the substantial visual appearance discrepancies between daytime and nighttime scenes. Existing fully supervised methods rely heavily on labor-intensive annotations, which are costly and exhibit limited generalization across domains. In this work, we investigate unsupervised day-night ReID and propose a novel framework that synergistically combines prompt learning and prototype-based representation learning to associate identities across domains without requiring manual labels. Our approach follows a progressive two-stage training strategy. In the first stage, we exploit the vision-language model to generate instance-specific textual prompts in an annotation-free manner. We employ an instance-level alignment mechanism to embed visual features and textual prompts into a unified semantic space, aligning unlabeled day/night images with learnable prompts via instance-aware dynamic-bias adaptation. In the second stage, we construct domain-specific prototype memory banks and introduce two complementary modules: i) an intra-domain identity association module to enhance feature discriminability within each domain, and ii) a cross-domain prototype matching module to reliably identify positive and negative prototype pairs, thereby establishing robust identity correspondences across day and night. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks validate the effectiveness of our method. Under the unsupervised setting, our framework attains Rank-1 accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art fully supervised methods.

</details>


### [95] [VOID: Defeating Unauthorized Mimicry in Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12263)
*Chunlin Qiu,Ang Li,Tianxiao Huang,Ruilin Gan,Yunjie Ge,Shenyi Zhang,Huayi Duan,Lingchen Zhao,Chao Shen,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为VOID的防御框架，用于防止潜在扩散模型（LDMs）被滥用于模仿个人身份。VOID通过操纵LDM的内在随机性，采用放大潜在编码误差和抵消目标引导信号两种新颖的扰动方式，实现语义损坏，从而有效阻止未经授权的模仿，同时保持视觉质量。实验评估表明，VOID的防御效果显著优于现有方法，将平均FID从113提升至365，改进幅度达223%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 

Method: 作者提出VOID防御框架，通过操纵LDM的内在随机性来解决问题。VOID采用两种新颖的扰动方式：1）放大潜在编码误差，以破坏图像的语义结构；2）抵消目标引导信号，以抑制模型的恢复能力。这种方法导致语义损坏，从而阻止任何未经授权的模仿。同时，VOID将扰动限制在受保护图像的人类不可感知区域，确保视觉效用不受影响。

Result: 作者在5个数据集上针对10种模仿攻击，对24种最先进的防御方法进行了全面评估。结果表明，VOID具有前所未有的保护能力：它将平均Fréchet起始距离（FID）从113提升至365，相比目前最强的防御方法实现了223%的改进。这证明VOID在阻止模仿的同时，有效保持了生成图像的质量。

Conclusion: VOID是一种有效的防御框架，能够克服现有LDM防御方法的局限性。通过操纵模型的内在随机性，VOID在阻止未经授权模仿方面表现出色，同时保持了图像的视觉效用。实验结果证明了其优越性，为保护个人身份免受LDM滥用提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: While Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) have revolutionized visual synthesis, they are increasingly exploited for unauthorized mimicry of individuals. Existing defenses inject deceptive perturbations to steer the generated images toward irrelevant targets. However, this approach hinges on an ungrounded assumption: subtle perturbations can maintain their deceptive efficacy throughout an LDM's extensive generation process. In reality, the model's innate restoration mechanism will remove such perturbations and cause individual identities to re-emerge in the images generated.
  We propose VOID, a defense framework that overcomes this conundrum by manipulating an LDM's intrinsic stochasticity. VOID perturbs the diffusion pipeline in two novel ways: 1) amplifying the latent encoding errors to shatter an image's semantic structure, and 2) counteracting the target guidance signals to suppress the model's restoration capabilities. This results in a semantic corruption that thwarts any unauthorized mimicry. Notably, the security gain does not come at the price of visual utility, as VOID simultaneously manages to confine perturbations to human-imperceptible regions of protected images. Our comprehensive evaluation of 24 state-of-the-art defenses against 10 mimicry attacks on 5 datasets demonstrates VOID's unprecedented protection power: it increases the average Frechet Inception Distance (FID) from 113 to 365, a 223% improvement over the strongest defense to date.

</details>


### [96] [Finding Sparse Subnetworks in One Training Cycle via Progressive Magnitude-Based Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12278)
*Romana Qureshi,Hafida Benhidour,Said Kerrache,Nahlah Aljeraisy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于幅度的渐进式剪枝方法，作为需要多次完整训练的彩票假设（LTH）的单循环替代方案。该方法在训练过程中通过线性调度逐渐增加稀疏度，并根据活跃权重幅度更新剪枝掩码。在CIFAR-10和MNIST数据集上对ResNet、VGG和LeNet架构的实验表明，该方法在高稀疏度下达到了与基准方法相当或更优的准确率，证明其是一种有效的神经网络稀疏化单循环方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经网络剪枝技术，特别是彩票假设（LTH），虽然证明了稀疏子网络可以匹配密集网络的性能，但其迭代剪枝过程需要多次完整的训练周期，计算成本高昂。因此，研究动机是评估一种高效的、只需单次训练周期的替代剪枝策略。

Method: 采用渐进式基于幅度的剪枝方法。该方法在训练过程中使用线性调度逐渐增加网络的稀疏度，并基于当前活跃权重的幅度来动态更新剪枝掩码。这是一种单周期方法，仅需一次训练过程。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和MNIST数据集上，使用ResNet、VGG风格和LeNet架构进行了系统实验，并与LTH、SNIP和GraSP等基线方法进行了比较。结果表明：在CIFAR-10上，该方法在ResNet-18（72.9%稀疏度）上达到95.12%的准确率，高于LTH报告的90.5%；在极端稀疏度下，该方法在类VGG架构（97%稀疏度）上达到93.13%的准确率，优于SNIP（约92.0%），在VGG-19（97.97%稀疏度）上达到93.44%的准确率，优于GraSP（92.19%）。对ResNet-18的稀疏度-准确率分析显示，在70-85%的稀疏度范围内，准确率与密集基线相比波动不超过0.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 在所评估的设置下，渐进式基于幅度的剪枝提供了一种有效的单周期神经网络稀疏化方法。它在保持预测性能的同时，显著降低了计算开销，为实际应用提供了一种更高效的模型压缩替代方案。

Abstract: Neural network pruning reduces model size by removing less important parameters while aiming to preserve predictive performance. Although the Lottery Ticket Hypothesis (LTH) shows that sparse subnetworks can match dense networks when trained from suitable initializations, its iterative pruning procedure requires multiple complete training cycles. This work evaluates progressive magnitude-based pruning as a single-cycle alternative. The method gradually increases sparsity during training using a linear schedule and updates pruning masks based on active weight magnitudes. We conduct systematic experiments on CIFAR-10 and MNIST across ResNet, VGG-style, and LeNet architectures, comparing the proposed method with representative iterative and initialization-based pruning baselines, including LTH, SNIP, and GraSP. On CIFAR-10, the method achieves 95.12\% accuracy on ResNet-18 at 72.9\% sparsity, compared with 90.5\% reported for LTH. At extreme sparsity, it achieves 93.13\% accuracy on a VGG-like architecture at 97\% sparsity, compared with approximately 92.0\% for SNIP, and 93.44\% accuracy on VGG-19 at 97.97\% sparsity, compared with 92.19\% for GraSP at 98\% sparsity. A sparsity-accuracy analysis on ResNet-18 further shows that accuracy remains within 0.1 percentage points of the dense baseline across 70--85\% sparsity. These results indicate that progressive magnitude-based pruning provides an effective single-cycle approach for neural network sparsification under the evaluated settings.

</details>


### [97] [CellNet -- Localizing Cells using Sparse and Noisy Point Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12286)
*Benjamin Eckhardt,Dmytro Fishman,Stuart Fawke,Andrew Curtis,Bo Fussing,Constantin Pape*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于回归的深度学习方法，用于在相位对比显微镜图像中使用稀疏点注释自动检测和计数活细胞，以支持大规模饱和基因组编辑筛选，该方法在低数据量情况下是零样本方法的有效替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生物学研究（尤其是大规模饱和基因组编辑筛选）中，反复计数大量活细胞是一个关键步骤。手动计数效率低下，而基于计算机视觉的自动化对于实现高通量和资源效率至关重要。然而，训练深度学习模型通常需要大量的密集标注数据，这成为实际应用中的主要瓶颈。

Method: 研究开发了一种回归导向的深度学习计算机视觉算法，专门用于在相位对比显微镜图像中检测和计数细胞。核心创新在于仅使用快速且易于获取的稀疏点注释（而非密集的像素级或物体级标注）进行模型训练，从而显著减少了标注工作量。该方法与当前最先进的零样本计数方法进行了性能对比。

Result: 实验结果表明，在训练数据有限（低数据量）的情况下，所提出的基于回归的计数方法表现出良好的性能，成为零样本方法的一个有前景的替代方案。该方法能够在减少标注需求的同时，实现对活细胞的有效自动计数。

Conclusion: 该研究成功开发了一种利用稀疏点注释自动计数相位对比显微镜图像中活细胞的方法。该方法降低了深度学习模型对大规模标注数据的依赖，提高了实用性，为支持人类基因组等领域的高通量生物研究做出了贡献。相关代码已公开。

Abstract: Counting living cells is an important step in many biological research workflows. Our collaborators at the Wellcome Sanger Institute study vital genes in humans via large scale saturation genome editing screening, which requires repeatedly counting cells a great number of times. Computer Vision based automation is crucial for high throughput and resource efficiency. In this work, we develop a regression-based deep learning computer vision algorithm to detect and count cells in phase-contrast microscopy images. To reduce annotation effort, which in practice often becomes a bottleneck, we focus on counting cells only using sparse point annotations, which are fast and easy to acquire. By comparison to state-of-the-art 0-shot methods, we show that regression-based counting is a promising alternative in low data regimes. Through developing methods to automatically count living cells in microscopy images, we contribute to valuable research on the human genome. The code is available at https://github.com/beijn/cellnet.

</details>


### [98] [Bridging the Modality Gap in Forensic Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12294)
*Ricardo González-Gazapo,Annette Morales-González,Yoanna Martínez-Díaz,Heydi Méndez-Vázquez,Milton García-Borroto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个统一的多模态检索框架，用于处理四项关键的法医任务（纹身图像检索、文本描述纹身检索、手绘草图纹身检索、面部草图检索），并证明通过融合视觉与文本模态能显著提升检索精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自动化图像检索在法医分析中日益重要，但现有研究主要关注多模态检索系统的开发与优化，而较少关注评估这些技术在多种真实法医场景中的适用性。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探索现代多模态大语言模型在操作化传统依赖人工专家的法医任务上的潜力。

Method: 提出一个统一的检索框架：1) 为所有查询和图像库自动生成结构化文本描述（使用多模态大语言模型）；2) 使用句子转换器进行文本嵌入与比较；3) 评估三种策略：仅视觉嵌入、仅文本嵌入，以及融合文本相似性分数与先进视觉特征提取器生成的图像相似性分数的多模态策略。

Result: 模态融合策略在检索精度和鲁棒性方面带来一致性提升，尤其在视觉信息有限或嘈杂的场景（如草图、部分纹身、片段化证人陈述）中效果显著。

Conclusion: 该研究证实了统一流程的法医应用价值，展示了现代多模态大语言模型如何操作化传统依赖人工专家分析的法医任务，使多模态检索成为支持涉及纹身、面部合成和证人描述调查工作的有力工具。

Abstract: Automated image retrieval plays an increasingly critical role in modern forensic analysis, supporting investigative workflows that rely on efficient comparison of visual evidence. While prior work has focused primarily on developing and optimizing multimodal retrieval systems, limited attention has been paid to evaluating the forensic applicability of these technologies across diverse real-world scenarios. In this study, we present a unified retrieval framework adapted to four key forensic tasks: (1) tattoo image retrieval given a tattoo query image; (2) tattoo retrieval guided by human-expert textual descriptions, modelling the common situation where a witness verbally describes a tattoo; (3) tattoo retrieval from hand-drawn sketches; and (4) face retrieval from forensic face sketches. Our system leverages a multimodal large language model (MLLM) to automatically generate structured textual descriptions for all queries and gallery images, followed by sentence-transformer embedding for text-based comparison. We evaluate retrieval using visual-only embeddings, text-only embeddings and a multimodal fusion strategy that combines text- and image-based similarity scores derived from state-of-the-art visual feature extractors relevant to each task. The fusion of modalities consistently improves retrieval precision and robustness, especially in scenarios where visual information is limited or noisy (e.g., sketches, partial tattoos, or fragmented witness statements). This work highlights the forensic value of a unified multimodal retrieval pipeline and demonstrates how modern MLLMs can operationalize challenging forensic tasks that traditionally rely on manual expert analysis. Our results position multimodal retrieval as a promising tool for supporting investigative workflows involving tattoos, facial composites, and witness descriptions.

</details>


### [99] [Findings of the MAGMaR 2026 Shared Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12295)
*Alexander Martin,Dengjia Zhang,Joel Brogan,Francis Ferraro,Jeremy Gwinnup,Reno Kriz,Teng Long,Kenton Murray,Andrew Yates,Xiang Xiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文概述了第二届MAGMaR研讨会中关于“通过多模态检索实现多模态增强生成”的共享任务结果，任务分为视频检索和基于检索视频的接地文章生成两个子任务，所有提交系统均表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在介绍第二届多模态增强生成通过多模态检索（MAGMaR）研讨会的共享任务结果，以促进多模态检索与生成领域的技术发展与评估。

Method: 共享任务包含两个子任务：(i) 视频检索；(ii) 基于检索到的视频接地生成文章。参赛团队可选择参加任一任务。检索任务有2个团队提交17个系统，生成任务有4个团队提交16个系统。

Result: 在检索任务中，所有17个系统均超越了基于去年共享任务获胜者构建的基线。在生成任务中，所有团队至少有一篇生成的报告被人类标注者评为最佳。

Conclusion: 第二届MAGMaR共享任务取得了积极成果，所有参与系统均表现出色，表明在多模态检索与接地生成领域取得了显著进展。

Abstract: This overview paper presents the results of the shared task for the second workshop on Multimodal Augmented Generation via Multimodal Retrieval (MAGMaR). In this shared task participants submitted systems focused on either (i) video retrieval or (ii) grounded generation of articles given retrieved videos. Teams could submit to either task. For the retrieval task, we had 2 participating teams that submitted a total of 17 systems -- all of which beat a baseline derived from the winner of last year's shared task. On the generation side, we had 4 teams submit 16 systems. All teams had at least one generated report that was labeled the best by a human annotator.

</details>


### [100] [Natural-Language Temporal Grounding in Hour-Long Videos is a Search Problem: A Benchmark and Empirical Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12300)
*Sukmin Seo,Geewook Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究小时级长视频的时间定位问题，指出当前Video-LLMs的主要瓶颈在于搜索而非识别。作者提出了ExtremeWhenBench基准测试，并证明了基于检索的混合方法能显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间定位研究主要针对短视频，而小时级长视频的自然语言定位问题尚未得到充分探索。作者认为在小时级视频中，关键挑战是搜索效率而非事件识别，需要新的方法和基准来研究这一问题。

Method: 作者提出了ExtremeWhenBench基准测试，包含2,273个查询和194个视频（平均时长75.7分钟，最长9小时），并采用开放形式的查询分布。在此基础上，他们设计了"检索然后定位"的混合方法。

Result: 实验发现所有现有开放Video-LLMs在该基准上表现不佳，而简单的帧级检索基线表现更好。失败分析显示85%的失败归因于搜索问题。所提出的混合方法相比单一Video-LLM提升了6.7倍的性能。

Conclusion: 在小时级长视频时间定位中，搜索效率是关键瓶颈。基于检索的混合方法能够有效解决这一问题，类似于开放域问答中的"检索然后阅读"策略，为长视频理解提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Temporal grounding--returning the interval $[t_s, t_e]$ for a natural-language query over a video--is the language interface to long-form video, yet has been studied on short videos; the dynamics of hour-scale natural-language grounding remain underexplored. We take the position that at hour-scale, the binding constraint is search, not recognition: Video-LLMs are bottlenecked not by localizing a nearby event, but--given a natural-language query--by searching for the relevant region of a long video. To test this, we release ExtremeWhenBench, the first open hour-scale grounding benchmark (2,273 queries over 194 videos, mean 75.7 min, max 9 hr) with an open-form query distribution. Every open Video-LLM collapses while a frame-level retrieval baseline outperforms them; a failure taxonomy attributes 85% of failures to search; and a retrieve-then-ground hybrid recovers 6.7x over the monolithic Video-LLM--mirroring retrieve-then-read in open-domain QA.

</details>


### [101] [From 2D Grids to 1D Tokens: Reforming Shared Representations for Multimodal Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12303)
*Yuchen Xian,Yunqiu Xu,Yang He,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对多模态图像融合中全局外观一致性难以保证的问题，本文提出一种基于预训练图像标记器的1D令牌接口，并设计了选择性令牌编辑机制，以轻量级方式协调全局外观，同时保持局部细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在2D特征网格上构建共享表示，虽能有效建模局部结构，但难以控制图像级的全局外观因素（如亮度、对比度、风格）。本文旨在通过引入紧凑的1D令牌空间来显式建模这些非局部因素，以平衡局部细节保持与全局外观一致性。

Method: 核心是构建一个基于冻结预训练图像标记器的1D令牌接口，用作全局信息载体。关键创新是选择性令牌编辑，该机制仅稀疏地更新或替换一小组关键令牌，从而在无需改变融合主干网络或引入额外损失函数的情况下，引导融合图像的全局外观一致性。

Result: 在四个常用的多模态图像融合基准数据集上进行了实验。结果表明，该方法在整体性能上达到了最佳水平，在全局外观一致性和局部细节保真度等多个评估指标上均取得了稳定且显著的提升。

Conclusion: 本文成功引入了一种新颖的1D令牌接口和选择性令牌编辑机制，为多模态图像融合提供了一种轻量且有效的全局外观调控方法。该方法在保持局部结构细节的同时，显著提升了融合图像的全局一致性，为平衡局部与全局优化目标提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Multimodal image fusion aims to integrate complementary information from different modalities into a fused image that preserves rich local details while maintaining globally consistent appearance. Existing approaches build shared representations on 2D feature grids, which excel at modeling local structures but offer limited leverage over image-level global appearance factors. To balance these objectives, we introduce a compact 1D token interface based on a frozen pretrained image tokenizer for modeling non-local appearance/base factors. Rather than using the tokenizer as a reconstruction backbone, our design uses the 1D token space as a global carrier while retaining the 2D spatial pathway for local structure restoration. Specifically, we introduce Selective Token Editing (STE), which sparsely updates/replaces a small set of critical tokens, providing a lightweight mechanism to steer global appearance coherence while keeping the fusion backbone unchanged and avoiding extra losses. Experiments on four commonly used benchmarks show that our method achieves the best overall performance, with consistent, multi-metric improvements in both global coherence and local fidelity. Project page: https://zju-xyc.github.io/1D-Fusion-Project-Page/

</details>


### [102] [Slots, Transitions, Loops: Learning Composable World Models for ARC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12316)
*Gege Gao,Bernhard Schölkopf,Andreas Geiger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Loop-OWM是一种用于抽象推理语料库（ARC）任务的以对象为中心的世界建模架构，它将规则学习为视觉-符号状态上的可组合转换，在ARC-1和ARC-2数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将ARC规则表达为语言描述、代码或符号程序，但ARC任务本质上是视觉-符号的，其规则表现为网格中对象、颜色、形状和空间关系的转换。需要一种能够直接学习这种视觉-符号转换的方法。

Method: Loop-OWM架构结合了颜色原型槽、演示条件任务摘要以及一个循环转换模型。该模型通过密集传播和槽条件校正来学习结构化状态间的可组合转换。

Result: 在ARC-1和ARC-2数据集上，Loop-OWM在参数数量相当或更少的情况下，性能优于非循环和循环基线模型。

Conclusion: ARC规则不仅可以作为语言描述或搜索程序来学习，还可以作为视觉-符号世界状态上的转换来有效学习。

Abstract: ARC tests in-context rule induction: given a few input-output demonstrations, a model must infer the hidden rule and apply it to a new query. While many approaches express ARC rules through language, code, or symbolic programs, ARC itself is visual-symbolic: rules appear as grid transitions over objects, colors, shapes, and spatial relations. We introduce Loop-OWM, an object-centric world-modeling architecture that learns these rules as composable transitions over structured states. It combines color-prototype slots, demonstration-conditioned task summaries, and a looped transition model with dense propagation and slot-conditioned correction. On both ARC-1 and ARC-2, Loop-OWM outperforms non-looped and looped baselines with comparable or fewer parameters. These results suggest that ARC rules can be learned not only as language descriptions or searched programs, but also as transitions over visual-symbolic world states.

</details>


### [103] [Anatomically Conditioned Recurrent Refinement for Topology-Aware Circle of Willis Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12319)
*Juraj Perić,Marija Habijan,Dario Mužević,Irena Galić,Danilo Babin,Aleksandra Pižurica*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Segmenting the Circle of Willis (CoW) from Magnetic Resonance Angiography (MRA) is challenging due to complex topology and thin vascular structures that are prone to fragmentation. Standard Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) often fail to capture these topological constraints, resulting in "broken vessel" artifacts. To address this, we propose the Anatomically Conditioned Recurrent Refinement U-Net (AC2RUNet). Our architecture decouples segmentation into two streams: a Static Stream that extracts invariant anatomical features and a lightweight Dynamic Stream that iteratively refines topological errors over time. We further introduce a dynamic curriculum learning strategy that transitions from high-recall geometric supervision to topology-aware constraints. Validated on the TopCoW dataset, AC2RUNet substantially reduces Hausdorff Distance (4.72 mm vs 9.17 mm) and Betti number errors (0.19 vs 0.40), improving topological connectivity over the nnU-Net baseline while maintaining comparable volumetric Dice.

</details>


### [104] [Echoes of the Prior: A Computational Phenomenology of Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12340)
*Gege Gao,Bernhard Schölkopf,Andreas Geiger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为“Echoes of the Prior”的互动艺术装置，通过模拟神经网络的突触退化，来艺术化地类比和可视化人类记忆消退时主观体验的混乱与瓦解过程，并将神经网络定位为一个认知代理，以探索神经形态美学在表现智能脆弱性方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该作品的创作动机源于对记忆本质的思考：记忆不仅是数据存储，更是构建我们现实认知的脚手架。当记忆衰退，世界会陷入无法辨认的混沌。因此，作者旨在通过一个艺术装置，来可视化这种遗忘的主观现象学，并推动一个新领域——神经形态美学——的发展，以探索表现智能脆弱性的新途径。

Method: 研究方法的核心是将神经网络，具体是一个前馈3D重建模型，用作艺术创作的“认知代理”。作者在该模型中诱导一种受控的“突触退化”过程，以此作为生物大脑“预测先验”（predictive priors）侵蚀过程的艺术类比。装置被设计为互动式，允许观众参与体验。

Result: 主要成果在于成功地将神经网络重新定位：它不再仅仅是工程工具，而是一个能够引发情感共鸣的认知代理。这个“硅基大脑”的结构退化过程，能够有效唤起人们对记忆丧失时那种迷失方向、充满诗意却又令人恐惧的主观体验的感知。作品提供了一个具体的、可互动的案例框架。

Conclusion: 作者认为，这项工作不仅是一个具体的艺术装置，更是一个催化剂。它为更广泛的学术和艺术社区提供了一个探索“神经形态美学”的框架，鼓励大家利用神经网络的退化与变异，来可视化和探讨智能、认知及现实感知的内在脆弱性。

Abstract: Memory is not merely the storage of data; it is the scaffolding of reality. When biological memory fades, the world does not simply turn black; it regresses into an unrecognizable chaos. Echoes of the Prior is an interactive installation that attempts to visualize this subjective phenomenology of forgetting. By inducing controlled synaptic decay within a Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction model, we create an artistic analogy for the erosion of the brain's predictive priors. We position the Neural Network not as a tool for engineering, but as a cognitive proxy - a silicon brain whose structural degeneration evokes the disorienting, poetic, and terrifying experience of losing one's grip on the world. Ultimately, we offer this framework as a catalyst, inviting the wider community to explore the uncharted potential of neuromorphic aesthetics in visualizing the fragility of intelligence. Interactive demo see https://decart-4d.github.io/.

</details>


### [105] [Atlas H&E-TME: Scalable AI-Based Tissue Profiling at Expert Pathologist-Level Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12346)
*Kai Standvoss,Miriam Hägele,Rosemarie Krupar,Julika Ribbat-Idel,Jennifer Altschüler,Gerrit Erdmann,Hans Pinckaers,Evelyn Ramberger,Madleen Drinkwitz,Ádám Nárai,Alexander Möllers,Katja Lingelbach,Sebastian Kons,Lukas Hönig,Recepcan Adigüzel,Joana Baião,Alberto Megina Gonzalo,Marius Teodorescu,Marie-Lisa Eich,Paolo Chetta,Shakil Merchant,Verena Aumiller,Simon Schallenberg,Andrew Norgan,Klaus-Robert Müller,Lukas Ruff,Maximilian Alber,Frederick Klauschen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Atlas H&E-TME，这是一个基于病理学基础模型的AI系统，能够从常规的H&E染色全切片图像中自动提取超过4500个定量指标，用于表征肿瘤及其微环境。该系统通过结合IHC信息的深度验证和覆盖广泛病例的广度验证，证明了其性能可匹配甚至超越仅依赖H&E图像的病理医生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: H&E染色分析是病理学的基石，但对其全切片图像进行可扩展的定量分析仍是核心挑战。验证此类AI系统面临根本性困难：仅基于H&E的参考标准存在形态学模糊性，而结合免疫组化（IHC）等更可靠方法的验证方案又难以大规模开展。因此，需要一个能够克服这些验证障碍并实现稳健、可扩展分析的系统。

Method: 1. **系统开发**：构建了Atlas H&E-TME系统，基于Atlas病理学基础模型，能够预测组织质量、组织区域和细胞类型标签。2. **双重验证框架**：为克服验证挑战，提出了结合深度与广度的验证策略。深度验证方面，设计了一种IHC引导的多病理学家共识协议，以生成分子水平上更可靠的参考标准。广度验证方面，在超过20万例高置信度的仅H&E病理学家标注数据（涵盖1500+病例、8种癌症类型及其转移灶、来自25+数据源和8+扫描仪）上对系统进行了基准测试。

Result: 与基于IHC的共识标准相比，Atlas H&E-TME系统的性能匹配或超过了仅使用H&E图像的病理医生。该系统在覆盖广泛形态变异和技术差异（不同癌症亚型、转移部位、数据来源和扫描仪）的广度基准测试中，表现出一致且稳健的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Atlas H&E-TME系统成功将病理学中最普遍的H&E染色数据转化为一个可扩展、定量的肿瘤及其微环境分析工具。这为转化研究和临床研究中下一代基于组织的生物标志物奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: Hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) staining is the cornerstone of histopathology, yet scalable, quantitative analysis of H&E whole-slide images (WSIs) remains a central challenge in computational pathology. We present Atlas H&E-TME, an AI-based system built on the Atlas family of pathology foundation models that predicts tissue quality, tissue region, and cell type labels across multiple cancer types, yielding over 4,500 quantitative readouts per slide at cell-level resolution. A key challenge to validating such systems is overcoming morphological ambiguity inherent to H&E-only ground truth and the limited scalability of more informed references drawing on modalities such as immunohistochemistry (IHC). We address this with a dual validation framework combining biologically grounded depth with technical and morphological breadth. For depth, we propose an IHC-informed multi-pathologist consensus protocol that substantially improves inter-rater agreement over conventional H&E-only annotation. This yields a molecularly grounded reference against which we compare Atlas H&E-TME and pathologists working from H&E alone. For breadth, we benchmark Atlas H&E-TME on over 200,000 high-confidence H&E-only pathologist annotations across 1,500+ cases spanning eight cancer types and their most common metastatic sites, with subtypes covering >90% of clinical cases per cancer type, drawn from 25+ sources and 8+ scanner models. Benchmarked against the IHC-informed consensus, Atlas H&E-TME matches or exceeds pathologist H&E-only performance and generalizes consistently and robustly across this broad morphological and technical scope. In doing so, Atlas H&E-TME turns the H&E slide -- the most ubiquitous data in pathology -- into a scalable, quantitative window into the tumor and its microenvironment, laying a foundation for the next generation of tissue-based biomarkers in translational and clinical research.

</details>


### [106] [DepthMaster: Unified Monocular Depth Estimation for Perspective and Panoramic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12368)
*Pengfei Wang,Shihao Wang,Liyi Chen,Zhiyuan Ma,Guowen Zhang,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DepthMaster是一个统一的度量深度估计框架，通过将全景图像分解为重叠的透视图块，结合对应一致性损失和虚拟投影相机先验，解决了跨不同相机类型（窄视场与全景）的泛化度量深度估计问题，在仅使用一个全景数据集训练的情况下，在13个多样化数据集上实现了最先进的零样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常针对特定相机类型量身定制，难以在多样化的设置中产生准确的、可泛化的度量深度估计。这源于两个关键挑战：透视相机和全景相机之间的固有几何差异，以及带有度量标注的全景训练数据的稀缺性。

Method: 引入DepthMaster统一框架。不使用专门的网络来学习球面畸变，而是将全景图像分解为重叠的透视图块。创新性地提出了对应一致性损失并注入虚拟投影相机作为几何先验，以实现图块间的无缝拼接，避免使用专用算子，并使骨干网络与标准Transformer设计保持高度兼容。该策略通过将所有输入统一为规范的透视表示来解决几何差异，并通过直接利用大量透视数据集中的强大度量先验来有效规避数据稀缺问题。

Result: 在仅包含一个全景数据集的混合数据集上进行训练，DepthMaster在13个多样化数据集上实现了最先进的零样本性能，不仅超越了通用方法，还在透视和全景领域均超越了领先的专用模型。

Conclusion: DepthMaster通过其统一的、基于分解和几何先验的方法，成功解决了跨不同相机类型的泛化度量深度估计难题，在数据稀缺的情况下取得了卓越的性能，证明了其在现实世界应用中的潜力。

Abstract: While monocular depth estimation has achieved significant progress, achieving generalized metric depth estimation for both narrow field-of-view (FoV) perspectives and $360^\circ$ panoramas remains an unsolved challenge. Existing methods are often tailored to specific camera types and struggle to produce accurate metric depth that generalizes across diverse settings. This limitation stems from two key challenges: the inherent geometric discrepancy between perspective and panoramic cameras, and the scarcity of panoramic training data with metric annotations. In this work, we introduce DepthMaster, a unified metric depth estimation framework. Rather than employing specialized networks to learn spherical distortions, we reformulate the problem by decomposing panoramic images into overlapping perspective patches. Crucially, distinct from prior projection-based methods that rely on ad-hoc architectural modifications to handle boundaries, we introduce a novel Correspondence Consistency Loss (CCL) and inject virtual projection cameras as geometric priors, allowing us to seamlessly stitch the patches while avoiding specialized operators and keeping the backbone largely compatible with standard Transformer designs. This strategy also resolves the geometric differences by unifying all inputs into a canonical perspective representation, and effectively circumvents data scarcity by directly unlocking powerful metric priors from vast perspective datasets. Trained on a mixed dataset that contains only one panorama dataset, DepthMaster achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on 13 diverse datasets, outperforming not only universal methods but also leading specialist models in both perspective and panoramic domains.

</details>


### [107] [A Turbo-Inference Strategy for Object Detection and Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12371)
*Zhen Zhao,Gang Zhang,Xiaolin Hu,Liang Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出turbo-inference策略，通过turbo-detection head和turbo-segmentation head实现物体检测与实例分割的迭代优化，提升两项任务的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实例分割方法多采用“先检测后分割”范式，检测精度直接影响分割性能，但实例分割任务对物体检测的反向促进作用研究较少。

Method: 设计turbo-detection head和turbo-segmentation head两个模块，构建任务间迭代通信的闭环，在推理阶段交替利用检测与分割结果的互补信息，无需重新训练模型。

Result: 在COCO、iFLYTEK和Cityscapes数据集上的实验表明，该方法显著提升了检测和分割精度，但增加了推理计算成本。

Conclusion: 所提方法在预测精度与推理速度之间实现了有效权衡，为联合优化检测与分割任务提供了新思路。

Abstract: Object detection and instance segmentation tasks are closely related. Existing top-down instance segmentation methods usually follow a detect-then-segment paradigm, where an initial detector is used to recognize and localize objects with bounding boxes, followed by the segmentation of an instance mask within each bounding box. In such methods, the detection accuracy directly influences the subsequent segmentation performance. However, previous research has seldom explored the impact of the instance segmentation task on object detection. In this paper, we present a turbo-inference strategy for the top-down methods that leverages the complementary information between detection and segmentation tasks iteratively. Specifically we design two modules: turbo-detection head and turbo-segmentation head, which facilitate communication between the tasks. The two modules form a closed loop that interlaces the detection and segmentation results without retraining the model. Comprehensive experiments on the COCO, iFLYTEK, and Cityscapes datasets demonstrate that our method substantially enhances both detection and segmentation accuracies with a certain increase in computational cost. The proposed method represents a tradeoff between prediction accuracy and inference speed. Codes are available at https://github.com/zhaozhen2333/Turbo-Learning.git.

</details>


### [108] [Illumination-Robust Camera-Based Heart-Rate Estimation for Physiological Sensing in Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12378)
*Zhi Wei Xu,Torbjörn E. M. Nordling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个端到端的空间-时间transformer框架，用于在光照变化的条件下从RGB摄像头进行非接触式心率估计，通过整合多项创新技术实现了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人和辅助机器人在日常环境中与人类交互时需要生理意识，但光照变化是远程光电容积描记法(rPPG)稳健部署的主要障碍。需要开发能够在光照变化条件下可靠工作的远程心率估计方法。

Method: 提出的空间-时间transformer框架集成了基于PRNet的3D人脸对齐、剪辑级光照增强、残差时间标准化模块和受控的混合时频监督。训练目标结合了软移位皮尔逊波形损失和频谱Kullback-Leibler散度损失，其中权重β控制频域心率指导的贡献。

Result: 在覆盖三个光照水平的静态全水平混合协议上的实验表明，β=5在测试的beta设置中提供了最强的结果，实现了最佳运行HR平均绝对误差为0.79 bpm，HR相关性为0.982。与PhysFormer基线相比，HR MAE降低了93.6%，HR相关性从0.088提高到0.982。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法在光照变化条件下显著提升了远程心率估计的性能，使其在机器人应用中具有实际可用性。

Abstract: Physiological awareness is important for service, social, and assistive robots that interact with humans in everyday environments. Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) enables non-contact heart-rate (HR) estimation from an RGB camera, making it a promising sensing modality for robot-mounted vision systems. However, illumination variation remains a major barrier to robust deployment. This paper presents an end-to-end spatial-temporal transformer framework for remote HR estimation on a new dataset with varied illumination. Our estimator integrates PRNet-based 3D face alignment, clip-level illumination augmentation, the Residual Temporal Standardization Module, and controlled hybrid temporal-frequency supervision. The training objective combines a Soft-Shifted Pearson waveform loss with a spectral Kullback-Leibler divergence loss, where a tuned weight ($\mathbfβ$) controls the contribution of frequency-domain heart-rate guidance. Experiments on a static all-level mix protocol covering three illumination levels show that $\mathbfβ=5$ provides the strongest result among the tested beta settings, achieving a best-run HR mean absolute error (MAE) of 0.79 bpm and an HR correlation of 0.982. Compared with the PhysFormer baseline evaluated on our dataset, our estimator reduces HR MAE by 93.6 %, while increasing HR correlation from 0.088 to 0.982, making it usable when illumination varies.

</details>


### [109] [How Seemingly Inconsequential Design Choices Dictate Performance of LLMs in Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12407)
*Kian R. Weihrauch,Thomas A. Buckley,William Lotter,Arjun K. Manrai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这篇论文通过系统性因子分析，揭示了在评估通用大语言模型（LLMs）处理病理学全切片图像（WSIs）任务时，之前研究中输入设计（如图像块大小、数量、放大倍数）的非优化选择，导致人为低估了通用模型的潜力。优化这些设计后，通用LLMs的性能显著提升，缩小了与专用模型的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估通用LLMs在病理学WSI任务上的表现时，普遍采用小尺寸、高放大倍数的图像块并通过多数投票独立处理，且未系统评估这些“看似不重要”的设计选择。这导致了通用模型始终表现不佳，强化了必须依赖领域特定训练或架构适配的观点。本研究旨在通过系统分析，检验这些设计选择对评估结论的影响，纠正可能存在的偏差。

Method: 采用系统性因子分析法，对四个输入设计因素（推理模式、图像块大小、放大倍数和图像块数量）进行测试和评估。在MultiPathQA基准上，测试了不同组合配置对模型性能的影响。

Result: 研究发现，以往研究因采用非优化配置而夸大了专用模型与通用LLMs之间的差距。将配置调整为“单平衡配置”（大尺寸图像块、低放大倍数、联合处理）后，GPT-5在TCGA癌症类型分类任务上的准确率从15.1%提升至39.5%，在GTEx器官分类任务上从38.1%提升至62.9%。针对特定任务进一步优化后，准确率可分别达到43.9%和71.6%。该配置在另外两个模型和完全独立的CPTAC队列中也具有泛化能力，无需任务特定调优即可将Gemini 3 Flash的性能提升23.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 1. 之前评估通用LLMs用于病理学WSI任务的方法存在缺陷，非优化的输入配置导致对其性能的低估。
2. 输入设计因素（如图像块大小、放大倍数、数量和推理模式）对模型性能有显著影响，且存在最优配置。
3. 通用大语言模型在获得优化输入配置后，在WSI分析任务上的性能可以大幅提升，显著缩小甚至可能消除与领域专用模型之间的感知差距。
4. 未来的研究在评估LLMs在医学图像分析任务中的基线性能时，必须系统性地优化和报告输入设计选择。

Abstract: General-purpose large language models (LLMs) are routinely used as baselines when evaluating specialized pathology models on whole-slide images (WSIs). Because WSIs exceed contemporary model context limits, LLM baselines routinely use small, high-magnification patches processed independently via majority voting, without systematic evaluation of seemingly inconsequential design choices such as patch size, patch count, and magnification. Generalist LLMs have consistently underperformed specialized systems, reinforcing the perception that domain-specific training or architectural adaptation is necessary for pathology tasks involving WSIs. Here, we conduct a systematic factorial analysis of four input design factors: inference mode, patch size, magnification, and patch count. We demonstrate that prior studies have overstated the gap between specialized models and general-purpose LLMs by choosing non-optimized input configurations. On the MultiPathQA benchmark, switching to a single balanced configuration (large patches at lower magnification, processed jointly) raises GPT-5 from 15.1% to 39.5% on cancer-type classification (TCGA) and from 38.1% to 62.9% on organ classification (GTEx). Per-task optimization yields further gains up to 43.9% (TCGA) and 71.6% (GTEx). The same configuration generalizes to two other models and to a fully held-out CPTAC cohort, where it improves Gemini 3 Flash by 23.4 percentage points without any task-specific tuning.

</details>


### [110] [Reroute, Don't Remove: Recoverable Visual Token Routing for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12412)
*Cheng-Yu Yang,Shao-Yuan Lo,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Reroute，一种无需训练的插件，用于解决视觉语言模型中视觉令牌剪枝的不可逆性问题。它通过将永久丢弃替换为可恢复路由，在解码过程中动态保留有潜力的视觉令牌，从而在激进压缩下提升模型的视觉定位能力，同时保持一般视觉问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉令牌缩减方法采用“排序后丢弃”的范式，会永久移除部分视觉令牌。本文指出这种不可逆操作是脆弱的，因为视觉令牌的重要性会随着解码深度而变化：在某一阶段被排在后面的令牌可能在后续层中变得重要，尤其是在处理需要精确定位的查询时。因此，需要一种更灵活、可恢复的替代方案。

Method: 提出Reroute，一个无需训练的插件。在每个路由决策阶段，选定的视觉令牌通过解码器块，而被延迟的令牌则跳过该阶段，重新进入候选池，以供下一个路由决策使用。此方法复用现有的注意力分数排序规则和分阶段计划，保留了其增强的基础剪枝方法的理论TFLOPs和KV缓存预算类别。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5和Qwen基座模型上，对FastV、PDrop和Nüwa的变体进行实验。结果表明，在激进的令牌缩减下，Reroute改善了视觉定位任务的性能，同时保持了一般视觉问答（VQA）任务的性能。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型的令牌缩减不应仅被视为不可逆的剪枝，也可以被视为一种可恢复的路由。Reroute为这一范式提供了可行的实现方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) project images into hundreds to thousands of visual tokens, making decoder inference expensive in both attention computation and KV-cache memory. Existing visual-token reduction methods largely follow a rank-and-remove paradigm: they score visual tokens, keep a compact subset, and permanently discard the rest. We show that this irreversible action is fragile because visual-token importance changes across decoder depth; tokens ranked low at one stage may become relevant in later layers, especially for grounding-sensitive queries. We propose Reroute, a training-free plug-in that replaces removal with recoverable routing. At each routing stage, selected vision tokens pass through decoder blocks, while deferred tokens bypass the stage and re-enter the candidate pool at the next routing decision. Reroute reuses existing attention-score ranking rules and stage-wise schedules, preserving the theoretical TFLOPs and KV-cache budget class of the pruning method it augments. Across FastV, PDrop, and Nüwa variants on LLaVA-1.5 and Qwen backbones, reroute improves grounding under aggressive token reduction while maintaining general VQA performance. These results suggest that VLM token reduction should not be viewed only as irreversible pruning, but also as recoverable routing. The code can be found here: https://github.com/elmma/mllm-reroute/

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [MASK: Multi-Agent Semantic K-Scheduling for Risk-Sensitive 6G Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11249)
*Ahmet Gunhan Aydin,Elif Tugce Ceran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了MASK框架，旨在解决6G协同机器人系统在严格瞬时带宽限制下的协同控制问题。该框架通过语义调度和分布式编码，在资源受限条件下实现了接近无约束通信基线的性能，并展现出对数据包丢失的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现6G协同机器人愿景需要在物理无线信道的严格频谱限制下，协调高性能的协同控制。在实际的协同感知场景中，频谱资源被量化为有限的物理资源块或正交子载波，这使得所有智能体同时传输不可行。因此，需要在严格的瞬时带宽上限约束下，维持稳健且风险感知的协同。

Method: 提出多智能体语义K-调度（MASK）控制架构。核心机制是仲裁器辅助的语义信息门控（A-SIG），这是一种轻量级协调机制，通过基于本地计算的语义重要性分数，仅调度排名前K的智能体，从而强制实施硬接入约束。随后，将这些优先观测聚合为紧凑的潜状态，并由一个自监督的全局编码器驱动分布式策略，以在数据稀疏的情况下减轻尾部风险。

Result: 在多样化基准测试中评估表明，即使信道访问仅限于群体规模的一小部分，MASK的性能也能与通信无约束的基线相匹配。此外，该框架对数据包擦除表现出固有的弹性，验证了语义调度作为资源受限6G系统关键使能技术的有效性。

Conclusion: 语义调度（MASK框架）是解决资源受限6G系统中协同机器人通信与控制问题的一种有效方法。它在严格的带宽限制下，通过优先传输高价值语义信息和分布式决策，实现了高性能、稳健且风险感知的协同控制，是支撑未来6G连接机器人愿景的关键技术。

Abstract: Realizing the vision of 6G connected robotics requires reconciling high-performance collaborative control with the rigid spectral limitations of physical wireless channels. In realistic collaborative sensing scenarios, spectral resources are quantized into finite physical resource blocks or orthogonal subcarriers, rendering simultaneous transmission by all agents infeasible. To address this, we propose Multi-Agent Semantic K-Scheduling (MASK), a control architecture designed to sustain robust, risk-aware coordination under strict instantaneous bandwidth caps. We introduce Arbiter-Assisted Semantic Information Gating (A-SIG), a lightweight coordination mechanism that enforces hard access constraints by scheduling only the top-K agents based on locally computed semantic importance scores. By aggregating these prioritized observations into a compact latent state, a self-supervised global encoder enables a distributional policy to mitigate tail risks despite data sparsity. We evaluate MASK across diverse benchmarks, demonstrating that it matches the performance of communication-unconstrained baselines even when channel access is restricted to a small fraction of the swarm size. Furthermore, the framework exhibits inherent resilience to packet erasures, validating semantic scheduling as a critical enabler for resource-constrained 6G systems.

</details>


### [112] [Model-based Optimization of Anguilliform Swimming Gaits for Soft Robotic Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11278)
*Brian Van Stratum,James Gallentine,Caleb Rucker,Eric Barth,Jonathan E. Clark,Kourosh Shoele*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了受七鳃鳗启发的软体双环境机器人SLIDER及其建模与优化设计流程，实现了水下高效游泳（系绳速度达21.7 cm/s），并探索了多模态运动优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种能够在水下（游泳）和陆地（攀爬）环境中运动的柔性机器人，并建立一套高效的建模与优化方法来指导其设计，以理解流体-结构相互作用并提升运动性能。

Method: 1. 流体建模：采用Lighthill大振幅细长体理论，整合惯性效应、涡力和粘性耗散。2. 结构建模：针对内部压力、尾部尺寸、体刚度等参数，开发并验证了一个快速的几何与材料非线性模型。3. 耦合求解：使用高效的二阶隐式箱式方法求解流固耦合方程。4. 实验验证：利用气动歧管系统在静水槽中驱动SLIDER机器人进行实验，以与计算结果交叉验证。5. 优化：结合高效模型与遗传算法，共同优化游泳控制模式和尾鳍设计（受攀爬形态约束）。

Result: 1. 揭示了游泳频率与主导力的关系：低频游泳受环境阻力主导，高频游泳则主要受流体惯性力影响。2. 优化后，系绳游泳速度达到21.7 ± 0.4 cm/s (0.59 BL/s)。3. 探索了针对同时执行游泳和攀爬任务的多模态机器人的优化流程。

Conclusion: 本文成功设计并优化了SLIDER软体双环境机器人，通过高效的流固耦合模型和优化算法显著提升了水下游泳性能，并阐明了频率对主导力学机制的影响。该研究为多模态软体机器人的设计提供了建模、优化方法和性能基准。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the Soft Lamprey-Inspired Dual Environment Robot (SLIDER) and a proper modeling and optimization procedure employed to design the robot. We represent the primary fluid environment actions - inertial effects, vortex forces, and viscous dissipation - using Lighthill's theory for large-amplitude elongated bodies. For structural design parameters such as internal pressure, tail size, and body stiffness, a fast, geometrically and materially nonlinear model is developed and validated. The fluid-structure interaction equations are solved implicitly with an efficient second-order box method. A pneumatic manifold robotic system is employed to actuate SLIDER in a quiescent water tank environment, allowing cross-comparison of computational and experimental results. We find that low-frequency swimming is dominated by resistant environmental forces, whereas higher-frequency swimming is primarily affected by inertial fluid forces. Using our efficient model alongside a genetic algorithm, we co-optimize a swimming control pattern and caudal fin design (subject to SLIDER's climbing morphology) to achieve a tethered swimming speed of 21.7 +/- 0.4 cm/s (0.59 Bl/s). Furthermore, we investigate the optimization procedure for a multimodal robot performing both swimming and climbing tasks.

</details>


### [113] [Embodied-R1.5: Evolving Physical Intelligence via Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11324)
*Yifu Yuan,Yaoting Huang,Xianze Yao,Yutong Li,Shuoheng Zhang,Linqi Han,Pengyi Li,Jiangeng Sun,Wenting Jia,Zhao Zhang,Yuhao Liu,Ruihao Liao,Yucheng Hu,Qiyu Wu,Yuxiao Li,Zibin Dong,Fei Ni,Yan Zheng,Shuyang Gu,Yi Ma,Hongyao Tang,Han Hu,Jianye Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Embodied-R1.5是一个拥有80亿参数的统一具身基础模型，它在单一架构中集成了具身认知、任务规划、纠正和指向等全面的具身推理能力。通过大规模数据系统（超过150亿token）和多任务平衡强化学习方法，该模型在24项具身视觉语言基准测试中的16项上达到了最优性能，超越了GPT-5.4等模型。它还能以少量数据微调成先进的视觉-语言-动作模型，并在真实机器人任务中表现出强大的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的具身智能模型通常功能单一，缺乏能够综合处理认知、规划和操作的统一架构。为了实现通用物理智能，需要构建一个能够内化多种具身能力、解决任务间冲突并支持长期任务自主执行的统一模型。

Method: 1. **数据构建**：设计三条自动化数据构建管线，大幅扩展关键能力的数据覆盖范围，构建了包含超过150亿token的大规模数据系统。  
2. **训练方法**：设计了一种多任务平衡强化学习方案，以缓解异质任务间的冲突。  
3. **架构框架**：引入了“规划-接地-纠正”闭环框架，使单一模型能够自主执行并自我纠正长期任务。  
4. **模型扩展**：利用内化的具身能力，仅需少量数据即可将基础模型微调为视觉-语言-动作模型。  
5. **评估与验证**：开发了针对具身任务的专用评估工具包EmbodiedEvalKit，并在多个基准测试和真实机器人上进行验证。

Result: 1. **基准测试性能**：在24项具身视觉语言基准测试中的16项上达到了当前最优水平，超越了如Gemini-Robotics-ER-1.5和GPT-5.4等领先模型。  
2. **VLA模型性能**：将基础模型微调为VLA模型后，在4个主流的操作基准套件上超越了如π0.5等领先的VLA模型。  
3. **真实世界验证**：在指令遵循、可供性接地、关节物体操作和长期复杂任务等真实机器人实验中表现出色，证明了其强大的物理世界泛化能力。

Conclusion: Embodied-R1.5成功构建了一个统一的具身基础模型，集成了多种核心具身推理能力，并通过创新的数据构建、训练框架和闭环执行机制，在模型规模相对较小（8B参数）的情况下，于多项基准和真实任务中取得了领先性能。该工作通过开源模型权重、数据集、训练代码和评估工具包，为具身基础模型领域的未来研究提供了重要资源和支持。

Abstract: We introduce Embodied-R1.5, a unified Embodied Foundation Model (EFM) that integrates comprehensive embodied reasoning capabilities, spanning embodied cognition, task planning, correction, and pointing, within a single architecture toward general physical intelligence. Leveraging three automated data construction pipelines to significantly expand the data coverage of critical capabilities, we build a large-scale data system of over 15B tokens, and design a multi-task balanced RL recipe to alleviate heterogeneous task conflicts. We further introduce a Planner-Grounder-Corrector (PGC) closed-loop framework that enables a single model to autonomously execute and self-correct over long-horizon tasks. With only 8B parameters, Embodied-R1.5 achieves SOTA on 16 out of 24 embodied VLM benchmarks, surpassing leading models like Gemini-Robotics-ER-1.5 and GPT-5.4. Benefiting from the internalized embodied capabilities, Embodied-R1.5 can be fine-tuned into a VLA with only a small amount of data, outperforming leading VLA models like $π_{0.5}$ across 4 popular manipulation benchmark suites. We further conduct extensive zero-shot real-robot experiments, validating performance in instruction following, affordance grounding, articulated object manipulation, and long-horizon complex tasks, demonstrating strong generalization to the physical world. We open-source model weights, datasets, training code, and EmbodiedEvalKit, an evaluation framework tailored for embodied tasks, to facilitate future research in EFMs.

</details>


### [114] [HiPi: Reproducible High-Fidelity Piezoresistive Sensors for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11372)
*Changyi Lin,Raihan Haque,Hui-Ping Wang,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HiPi系统，这是一种为机器人操作设计的可复制、高保真压阻式触觉传感系统，它解决了现有系统在可复制性与高保真读出之间的实践权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压阻式触觉传感器因其薄、轻、低成本和可扩展至密集大面积传感的特点，在机器人操作中具有吸引力。然而，现有系统面临一个实际的权衡：近期的可复制设计强调易用性和复制便捷性，而高保真读出架构在制造、组装和部署上仍然更困难。研究旨在弥合可复制制造与高保真读出之间的鸿沟。

Method: 基于低串扰读出原理，HiPi围绕可复制性、可部署性和多传感器可扩展性重新设计了整个硬件栈。系统包括：兼容商用PCB制造和组装服务的紧凑读出PCB，省去手动焊接；更小、更低成本的基于STM32的MCU模块；优化的通信流水线，在双手设置中实现四块密集触觉阵列（共2048个触觉单元）的220 Hz读出；以及基于FPCB的导电层，简化了传感器制造和堆叠。

Result: 实验使用结构化3D打印接触图案表明，HiPi显著优于可复制基线，更好地保留了接触几何形状。平均交并比（IoU）从0.428提高到0.797，平均Dice分数从0.539提高到0.886。

Conclusion: HiPi系统有效地弥合了可复制制造与高保真读出之间的重要差距，使密集压阻式触觉传感在双手操作和多手指机器人系统中的实际应用变得更加可行。

Abstract: Piezoresistive tactile sensors are attractive for robotic manipulation because they are thin, lightweight, low-cost, and scalable to dense large-area sensing. However, existing systems still face a practical trade-off: recent reproducible designs emphasize accessibility and ease of reproduction, whereas high-fidelity readout architectures remain more difficult to fabricate, assemble, and deploy. We present HiPi, a reproducible high-fidelity piezoresistive sensing system for robotic manipulation. Building on a low-crosstalk readout principle, HiPi redesigns the complete hardware stack around reproducibility, deployability, and multi-sensor scalability. The system includes a compact readout PCB compatible with commercial PCB fabrication and assembly services, eliminating manual soldering; a smaller and lower-cost STM32-based MCU module; an optimized communication pipeline that achieves 220 Hz readout in a bimanual setup with four dense tactile arrays (2048 taxels in total); and FPCB-based conductive layers that simplify sensor fabrication and stacking. Experiments with structured 3D-printed contact patterns show that HiPi preserves contact geometry substantially better than a reproducible baseline, improving the average IoU from 0.428 to 0.797 and the average Dice score from 0.539 to 0.886. These results suggest that HiPi bridges an important gap between reproducible fabrication and high-fidelity readout, making dense piezoresistive tactile sensing more practical for bimanual manipulation and multi-fingered robotic systems.

</details>


### [115] [PLUME: Probabilistic Latent Unified World Modeling and Parameter Estimation for Multi-Finger Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11396)
*Abhinav Kumar,Soshi Iba,Rana Soltani Zarrin,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PLUME是一种概率隐式统一世界模型，通过联合学习物理参数估计和系统动力学演化，解决灵巧操作中的不确定性，实现模拟到真实世界的高效零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指手灵巧操作对物体形状、姿态和摩擦系数等物理参数敏感。标准域随机化策略在精细任务（如螺丝刀转动）中可能不足，因为操作策略可能需要根据具体参数值进行调整。因此需要一种能处理部署时参数不确定性的方法。

Method: 提出PLUME（概率隐式统一世界模型与参数估计）框架。它联合学习一个隐式空间，用于共同表示多种物理参数及奖励（这些是部分可观测变量的函数），并基于这些参数条件化系统动力学演化。通过在线参数推断实现世界模型与真实动力学的对齐，而非重新训练或微调。

Result: 在模拟任务（螺丝刀转动、阀门转动、桶提升、圆盘弹射）和硬件螺丝刀转动任务上进行评估。成功实现了模拟训练策略的零样本迁移，并优于当前先进的离线强化学习和基于世界模型的行为克隆基线方法。

Conclusion: PLUME通过在线参数推断实现了世界模型与真实动力学的高效对齐，为处理灵巧操作中的物理参数不确定性提供了一种有效方法，能够实现从模拟到真实世界的零样本成功迁移。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation with multi-finger hands can be sensitive to physical parameters such as object shape, pose, and friction coefficients. While simulation enables large-scale data collection with known parameter values, simulation-trained policies must still handle uncertainty at deployment, where the true parameters and therefore the true dynamics are unknown. Standard domain randomization strategies may be insufficient for precise tasks like screwdriver turning, as manipulation strategies may need to change depending on specific parameter values. To address this, we propose Probabilistic Latent Unified world Modeling and parameter Estimation (PLUME), a world model that jointly learns to evolve a belief over parameter values as well as the system dynamics conditioned on those parameters. We learn a latent space to jointly represent multiple qualitatively different physical parameters along with rewards, themselves functions of partially-observable variables, to inform planning. Our novel learning framework leads to efficient alignment of the world model to true dynamics through online parameter inference as opposed to re-training or fine-tuning. We evaluate our method on simulated screwdriver turning, valve turning, bucket lifting, and disk flicking tasks, as well as a hardware screwdriver turning task, where we achieve successful zero-shot transfer of our simulation-trained policy and outperform state-of-the-art offline reinforcement learning and world-model-augmented behavior cloning baselines. Please see our website at https://plume-world-model.github.io for videos.

</details>


### [116] [Dynamic Execution Horizon Prediction for Chunk-based Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11408)
*Yuchi Zhao,Miroslav Bogdanovic,Arjun Sohal,Liyu Tao,Kourosh Darvish,Alán Aspuru-Guzik,Florian Shkurti,Animesh Garg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出动态执行时域预测（DEHP），通过在线强化学习为预训练的固定时域动作分块策略动态调整执行时域，显著提升机器人精细与长时程操作任务的成功率，并在精细阶段自动缩短执行时域以增强反应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器人策略（如扩散/视觉-语言-动作模型）广泛采用动作分块，其核心假设是执行时域固定，导致策略在分块执行期间处于开环状态。这对需要频繁重新规划的精细操作任务尤为不利。实践中，执行时域依赖经验调整且高度任务依赖，缺乏自动化和自适应机制。

Method: 提出动态执行时域预测（DEHP）方法：在冻结预训练分块策略（作为黑盒动作生成器）的同时，训练一个轻量级执行时域预测分支。该分支使用在线强化学习进行训练，根据当前状态预测最优的执行时域长度，从而实现时域的动态、任务自适应调整。

Result: 在多个高精度和长时程操作任务上的评估表明，DEHP大幅提升了任务成功率。定性分析显示，DEHP能在任务的精细操作阶段预测较短执行时域，在自由空间运动阶段预测较长时域，从而平衡了开环分块执行的效率与闭环控制的反应能力。

Conclusion: DEHP为现有动作分块策略提供了一种有效、轻量且即插即用的改进方案。它通过动态调整执行时域，解决了固定分块带来的开环局限性，无需重新训练基础策略，显著增强了机器人策略在复杂操作任务中的性能和适应性。

Abstract: Action chunking has become a standard design in modern robot policies, from diffusion/flow policies to vision-language-action models, where the policy predicts a sequence of actions and executes a fixed number of them instead of acting one step at a time. However, this paradigm relies on a key assumption: a fixed execution horizon. During chunk execution, the policy operates open-loop, which is particularly problematic for fine-grained manipulation tasks that require frequent replanning. In practice, the execution horizon is typically chosen through empirical tuning and is highly task-dependent. To this end, we propose Dynamic Execution Horizon Prediction (DEHP), an effective method that trains a lightweight execution-horizon prediction branch using online reinforcement learning while keeping the pretrained chunk policy completely frozen. This makes the method compatible with black-box chunk policies and isolates the effect of adapting the execution horizon from changes to the underlying action generator. Across our evaluations, DEHP improves the success rate of different high-precision and long-horizon manipulation tasks by a large margin. Our qualitative analysis further shows that DEHP predicts shorter execution horizons during fine-grained stages of the task and longer horizons during free-space motion. In this way, DEHP balances the efficiency of open-loop chunk execution with the reactivity of closed-loop single-step control. Project page: https://dehp-chunking.github.io/

</details>


### [117] [A Modular Dual-Camera Pipeline for Micro-Inspection Using Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11419)
*S. H. Mirtajadini,N. Rublein,R. M. Ramakrishnan,G. ter Maat,M. Aldibaja,A. Y. Mersha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为aerial_micro_inspection的通用空中微检查流水线，它利用配备变焦云台检查摄像头和广角立体导航摄像头的PX4无人机，并结合视觉反馈，以在无人机受干扰时实现稳健的检查覆盖，该方法在树木和温室的检查任务中得到了有效验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机检查系统存在几个关键局限：需要无人机飞近目标或执行复杂路径以捕捉细节；无人机飞行易受干扰和定位误差影响，在狭窄视野下可能导致目标丢失；轨迹规划通常需要目标几何、位置和朝向等先验信息，这对于树木、车辆或人等非结构化目标往往不可用。

Method: 该研究提出一个通用流水线，包括：1) 使用配备变焦云台检查摄像头和广视野立体导航摄像头的PX4无人机；2) 通过立体视觉在作业现场获取目标表面、估计距离并将其划分为更小的检查区域；3) 引入一个视觉反馈环路，以补偿无人机在检查摄像头巡视大表面的小分区时产生的运动。该流水线基于ROS 2开发，其表面分割和微目标检测模块可替换以适配新应用。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中进行了评估。仿真结果表明，该流水线在无人机存在干扰时提高了检查覆盖的稳健性。真实世界实验主要针对两个场景：树木检查（检测橡树 processionary 毛虫及其卵）和温室检查（检测粘性诱捕器上的白粉虱），结果证明其能有效检测目标并获取高细节图像。

Conclusion: aerial_micro_inspection流水线提供了一个开源、可适配的解决方案，以克服现有无人机微检查系统的挑战。它通过创新的双摄像头系统和视觉反馈机制，实现了在干扰下的稳健检查，并在非结构化环境的实际应用中展现了有效性。

Abstract: Most existing drone-based inspection systems require the drone to fly dangerously close to the target or follow complex flight paths to capture small details. In addition, drone flight is affected by disturbances and localization inaccuracies, which can cause the drone to lose sight of its supposed target when it has a narrow view. Furthermore, trajectory planning often requires prior information about the target's geometry, position, and orientation, which is not always available for non-structural targets such as trees, vehicles, or people. To address these challenges, this paper presents aerial_micro_inspection, a generic pipeline for aerial micro-inspection across different use cases. The pipeline assumes a PX4-powered drone equipped with two cameras: (i) a zoomed, gimbal-mounted inspection camera that captures fine details without requiring the drone to fly very close to the target, and (ii) a wide-field-of-view stereo navigation camera that acquires the target surface on site, estimates its range, and partitions it into smaller inspection regions. In addition, a vision-based feedback loop compensates for drone motion while the inspection camera visits small partitions of a larger surface. We evaluate the pipeline in simulation and real-world experiments, mainly in two use-case scenarios: tree inspection for detecting oak processionary caterpillars and their eggs, and greenhouse inspection of sticky traps for detecting whiteflies. The results show improved coverage robustness under drone disturbances in simulation, as well as effective detection of caterpillars and eggs and high-detail imaging of insects in real-world experiments. The pipeline is open-source, developed in ROS 2, and can be adapted to new applications by replacing the surface-segmentation and micro-target detection checkpoints. The code is available at: https://github.com/SaxionMechatronics/aerial_micro_inspection

</details>


### [118] [Bridging the sim2real gap in the table tennis robot with a transformer-based ball states predictor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11464)
*Yin Bi,Christian Conti,Bilan Yang,Alexander Sigrist,Peter Dürr,Naoya Takahashi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer的机器人乒乓球状态预测框架，通过注意力机制直接从历史观测中建模长期时间相关性，结合大规模真实数据集实现高精度长期预测，并引入SPAD策略以改善模拟到现实的策略迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人乒乓球是高速闭环控制的代表性任务，准确快速的球状态预测对可靠规划和控制至关重要。现有物理模型方法严重依赖精确参数和初始状态，而学习方法往往难以捕捉长期时间依赖，且通常基于有限或模拟数据训练，泛化能力受限。

Method: 1. 提出基于Transformer的乒乓球状态预测框架，利用注意力机制直接建模历史观测数据中的长期时间相关性，无需显式飞行或弹跳模型。
2. 收集大规模真实世界数据集，涵盖不同技能水平的球员和多样化的发球机配置，以支持稳健学习和泛化。
3. 引入即插即用的模拟到现实转移策略SPAD，在部署时用真实数据训练的预测器替换训练阶段使用的物理模拟器，无需重新训练。

Result: 1. 高容量Transformer架构与大规模真实数据的结合，实现了准确的长期预测。
2. SPAD策略通过简单替换模拟器，有效缩小了模拟与现实之间的差距。
3. 该方法在保持模拟训练高效性和可扩展性的同时，提升了策略的模拟到现实迁移能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的Transformer预测框架和SPAD转移策略为机器人乒乓球状态预测提供了有效解决方案，显著改善了预测准确性并提升了模拟到现实的策略迁移能力，为动态环境下的高速机器人控制任务提供了新思路。

Abstract: Robotic table tennis is a representative benchmark for high-speed, closed-loop robotic control in dynamic environments, where accurate and fast prediction of ball states is critical for reliable planning and control. Physics-based approaches rely heavily on accurate parameter identification and precise initial state, while learning-based methods often struggle to capture long-range temporal dependencies and are typically trained on limited or simulated data. We propose a transformer-based framework for table tennis ball state prediction that leverages attention mechanisms to model long-range temporal correlations directly from historical observations, without relying on explicit flight or bounce models. To support robust learning and generalization, we collected a large-scale real-world dataset from players of varying skill levels and diverse ball cannon configurations. The combination of a high-capacity transformer architecture and extensive real-world data enables accurate long-horizon forecasting. Building on this capability, we introduce a plug-and-play sim-to-real transfer strategy, Swap Predictor at Deployment (SPAD), which replaces the physics-based simulator used during training with the proposed real-world-trained predictor at deployment, improving the sim-to-real transferability of the policy without requiring retraining. We demonstrate that this simple substitution effectively narrows the sim-to-real gap while preserving the efficiency and scalability of simulation-based training.

</details>


### [119] [Steering Multirobot Behavior via Closed-Loop Affine Activation Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11489)
*Satyajeet Das,Darren Chiu,Shashank Hegde,Gaurav S. Sukhatme*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了CLAE框架，通过在推理时编辑冻结策略的中间激活来在线调整机器人行为，无需修改基础策略权重，有效避免了灾难性遗忘问题，并在多四旋翼导航任务中验证了其在控制个体行为、协调编队和生成新行为方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界机器人需要在预训练策略的范围之外调整行为。传统策略微调或重新训练存在灾难性遗忘风险，会降低基础性能。因此，需要一种推理时框架，在保持基础策略权重和下游动作头不变的前提下，通过编辑中间激活来灵活引导行为。

Method: CLAE是一个推理时框架，将行为引导视为闭环问题。其方法包括：1）在冻结策略的激活上训练稀疏自编码器；2）通过后验探测选择与行为相关的潜在特征；3）学习一个轻量级的基于强化学习的引导策略，在推理时对选定的潜在特征施加状态依赖的仿射编辑。该编辑过程根据机器人状态、环境、目标行为和多机器人上下文进行在线适应。

Result: 通过在冻结的多四旋翼导航策略上进行广泛的仿真和物理实验，CLAE展示了三项能力：1）通过控制每个机器人的速度剖面来引导单个机器人行为；2）通过保持所需编队来协调多机器人行为；3）生成全新的行为，即在导航至目标位置的同时，减少机器人暴露于环境中的监视摄像头下。

Conclusion: CLAE提供了一种有效、灵活且轻量级的推理时方法，能够引导冻结策略产生多样化的、适应性强的新行为，同时避免了重新训练带来的灾难性遗忘问题，在多机器人系统的行为适应与生成方面具有重要应用潜力。

Abstract: Real-world robots need to adapt their behavior beyond the envelope of their pre-trained policy. Policy finetuning or retraining are options, but they risk catastrophic forgetting, degrading the pretrained policy's base performance. To combat this, we introduce CLAE: Closed-Loop Affine Activation Editing, an inference-time framework for steering the behavior of a frozen policy by editing intermediate activations while keeping the base policy weights and downstream action head untouched. CLAE approaches behavior steering as a closed-loop problem whose outputs edit policy activations that adapt online to the robot state, environment, target behavior, and multi-robot context. It trains a sparse autoencoder over frozen-policy activations, selects behavior-relevant latent features via post-hoc probing, and learns a lightweight RL-based steering policy that applies state-dependent affine edits to selected latents during inference. We validate CLAE on a frozen multi-quadrotor navigation policy trained to perform a single task: navigating robots to a set of goal locations while avoiding obstacles. Through extensive simulations and physical tests, we show that while navigating to their goal positions, CLAE can 1. steer individual robot behavior by controlling each robot's velocity profile; 2. coordinate multirobot behavior by preserving a desired formation; and 3. produce entirely new behavior wherein robots are required to reduce their exposure to surveillance cameras in the environment.

</details>


### [120] [Learning Object Manipulation from Scratch via Contrastive Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11525)
*Tongle Shen,Caleb Chuck,Fan Feng,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 对比强化学习在交互丰富的机器人操作任务中因对象交互（如接触、抓取）导致的动态模式变化而表现不佳。本文将操作动力学建模为分段平滑马尔可夫过程，并提出交互加权重采样方法，通过交互感知的重采样保留模式边界以捕捉多模态和分段非线性可达性。该方法在仿真和真实世界机器人冰球任务中显著提升了样本效率和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管对比强化学习在运动控制和简单控制领域取得成功，但在交互丰富的操作任务中往往表现不佳。本文认为关键难点在于对象交互（如接触或抓取）会引发底层动态模式的显著变化，而标准对比强化学习的能量函数难以表示和规划这种由交互诱导的模式变化所形成的分段非线性可达结构。

Method: 首先，将操作动力学形式化为分段平滑马尔可夫过程，分析交互诱导的模式变化如何产生分段非线性可达结构。基于此分析，提出交互加权重采样方法：在交互前、交互中和交互后三个阶段进行交互感知的重采样，促使学习到的表示保留决定未来可达性的模式边界，从而能够捕捉多模态和分段非线性可达性。

Result: 在多个以交互为中心的环境中（包括2D动态控制、机器人操作和机器人冰球），IWR相比先前的对比强化学习方法提升了样本效率和整体性能，在仿真中平均改进19.8%。此外，通过sim-to-real流程，使用IWR训练的策略实现了首个能够击中目标的真实世界机器人冰球智能体，成功率从25%提升至60%。

Conclusion: 交互加权重采样方法通过显式建模交互诱导的模式变化和保留关键模式边界，有效解决了对比强化学习在交互丰富操作任务中的局限性，显著提升了样本效率、整体性能以及sim-to-real的迁移效果，为复杂机器人交互任务提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Contrastive Reinforcement Learning (CRL) has seen recent success in a wide variety of goal-conditioned robotics tasks by learning structured representations of the dynamics. However, despite its success in locomotion and simpler control domains, CRL often struggles in interaction-rich manipulation. We argue that a key source of this difficulty is object-centric interaction, such as contact or grasping, that induces distinct changes in the underlying dynamic modes. In this work, we formulate manipulation dynamics as a piecewise-smooth Markov process and show that interaction-induced mode changes create piecewise nonlinear reachability structures that are difficult for standard CRL energy functions to represent and plan over. Based on this analysis, we introduce Interaction-weighted Resampling (IWR). IWR performs interaction-aware resampling around phases before, during, and after interactions, encouraging the learned representation to preserve the mode boundaries that determine future reachability to capture multi-modal and piecewise nonlinear reachability. Across interaction-centric environments, including 2D dynamic control, robotic manipulation, and robot air hockey, IWR improves both sample efficiency and overall performance over prior CRL methods, with 19.8% average improvement in simulation. Finally, using a sim-to-real pipeline with policies trained by IWR, we demonstrate the first real-world goal-conditioned robot air hockey agent capable of hitting goals, improving success from 25% to 60%. Project Page: IWR-arxiv.github.io.

</details>


### [121] [Adversarial Attacks on Learned Policies for Surgical Robotic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11535)
*Shutong Jin,Ziyang Chen,Preethi Satish,Paavan Gupta,Florian T. Pokorny,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首次系统研究手术机器人学习策略面临的对抗性攻击风险，发现细微视觉扰动可严重干扰或操纵手术机器人策略，导致手术成功率平均下降61%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的策略正被用于增强机器人辅助手术中人类外科医生的灵巧性，但端到端视觉-动作映射可能面临对抗性攻击风险，导致患者受伤。需要评估这些学习策略的安全性与脆弱性。

Method: 提出两种威胁模式：破坏性攻击（中断策略执行）和转向攻击（引导策略朝特定方向行动）。设计了三种具有不同策略信息访问权限的对抗攻击方法，并在去骨术和缝合术两个手术子任务上进行评估。涵盖ACT、Diffusion Policy和Pi0三种端到端策略架构。引入了模仿自然视觉变化（如光照变化）的新型光度对抗攻击，生成有效且视觉上合理的扰动。通过使用幻影模型进行的560次物理实验进行验证。

Result: 最先进的手术机器人策略可被显著破坏，在物理实验中平均导致61%的手术子任务成功率下降。

Conclusion: 手术机器人学习策略面临严重的对抗性安全威胁，即使是细微、难以察觉的视觉扰动也能导致手术任务失败或错误操作。研究强调了在手术机器人系统中开发强大防御机制和对抗性鲁棒性的迫切需求。

Abstract: Learning-based policies are being considered to augment the dexterity of human surgeons in robot-assisted surgery. Can the end-to-end mapping from visual observations to robot actions be vulnerable to adversarial attacks, potentially leading to patient injury? In this paper, we present the first study of adversarial threats to learning-based policies in surgical robotics. We investigate two threat modes: (a) disruptive attacks, where imperceptible visual perturbations interrupt policy execution, and (b) steering attacks, where such perturbations steer policy actions toward attacker-specified directions. We formulate three adversarial attack methods, each with increasing access to policy information, and evaluate their impact on two surgical subtasks: debridement and suturing. Our evaluation covers three end-to-end policy architectures: ACT, Diffusion Policy, and Pi0. In addition, we introduce a new class of photometric adversarial attacks that mimic natural visual changes, such as lighting variations, to generate effective yet visually plausible perturbations. Results from 560 physical experiments using phantoms for debridement and suturing suggest that state-of-the-art policies can be significantly disrupted, resulting in an average 61% reduction in surgical subtask success rates. Project page: https://sites.google.com/view/adversary-surgery

</details>


### [122] [ConsistencyPlanner: Real-time Planning with Fast-Sampling Consistency Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11569)
*Qichao Zhang,Xing Fang,Jiaqi Fang,Zhenwen Cai,Jie Ling,Qiankun Yu,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Consistency Planner，一个基于快速采样一致性模型的实时规划框架，旨在通过高效多模态采样和异质特征融合技术，提升自动驾驶系统在复杂动态场景中的规划安全性与决策质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂现实驾驶场景中，传统基于规则的规划方法虽可解释，但预定义启发式规则缺乏对动态交通环境的适应性；而基于学习的方法尽管前景广阔，却难以在建模多样多模态驾驶行为与实现实时规划之间取得平衡，常导致动作犹豫或不安全。这构成了自动驾驶系统的关键挑战。

Method: 本文提出的方法基于两项关键技术：1）高效多模态采样：采用快速采样一致性模型生成多样且合理的未来轨迹，以高效探索多模态动作，克服了先前迭代生成方法的计算瓶颈。2）异质特征融合：引入注意力增强的解码器，动态整合场景特征和动作令牌等异质输入特征，形成统一表示，以支持鲁棒的规划。

Result: 在Waymax模拟器中进行的广泛评估表明，该方法在安全指标上优于现有方法，特别是在具有挑战性的动态场景中表现出色。

Conclusion: Consistency Planner通过结合快速采样一致性模型和注意力增强的异质特征融合，有效解决了实时多模态规划的难题，显著提升了自动驾驶规划的安全性和决策鲁棒性，为复杂动态场景下的自动驾驶系统提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Closed-loop planning in complex, real-world driving scenarios presents a critical challenge for autonomous driving systems. While traditional rule-based methods are interpretable, their predefined heuristics lack the adaptability for dynamic traffic environments. Learning-based approaches have shown considerable promise. Conversely, learning-based approaches, despite their promise, struggle to balance the modeling diverse and multimodal driving behaviors and real-time planning, often leading to indecisive or unsafe actions. To address this limitation, we propose Consistency Planner, a real-time planning framework with fast-sampling consistency models. Our approach is built upon two key technical contributions. Efficient Multimodal Sampling: We employ fast-sampling consistency models to generate a diverse set of plausible future trajectories. This enables efficient, real-time exploration of multimodal actions, overcoming the computational bottlenecks of previous iterative generative methods. Heterogeneous Feature Fusion: We introduce an attention-enhanced decoder that dynamically integrates heterogeneous input features (including scene feature and action token) into a cohesive representation for robust planning. Extensive evaluation in the Waymax simulator demonstrates superior performance in safety metrics compared to existing methods, with particularly strong results in challenging dynamic scenarios.

</details>


### [123] [Distortion-Resilient Robotic Imitation Learning for Autonomous Cable Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11577)
*Hao Wang,Fu-Zhao Ou,Shiqi Wang,Zhaolin Wan,Xiaopeng Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对机器人线缆布线任务中图像观测失真导致模型训练准确性和可靠性下降的问题，提出了一种新颖的模仿学习框架。该框架包含图像质量评估模块、基于置信度的学习机制和决策模块，能够在图像失真情况下保持高性能，实验表明其有效提升了决策模块的整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能控制方法的快速发展赋予了机器人强大的自主智能。线缆布线作为工业中普遍的基础任务，是对机器人灵巧性和序列决策能力的严格基准测试。在这些实际场景中，经常出现图像观测失真。以低质量图像观测为特征的样本常常阻碍精确的模型训练，对智能控制系统的可靠性和准确性构成挑战。然而，目前尚无专门针对图像信号失真场景的智能控制解决方案被提出。同时，图像质量信息也未被充分挖掘以进一步增强智能控制方法的性能。

Method: 为此，我们提出了一种新颖的机器人模仿学习框架，该框架包含一个图像质量评估模块、一个基于置信度的学习机制和一个决策模块，旨在即使在图像观测失真情况下也能保持高性能。在所提出的框架中，图像质量评估模块与基于置信度的学习机制协同作用，以增强决策模块的效能。具体而言，图像质量评估模块被整合用于从图像观测中提取图像质量信息，而基于置信度的学习机制则自适应地优先处理具有挑战性的样本，以提高学习效果。决策模块则确定适当的离散技能或连续动作。

Result: 实验结果表明，我们构建的框架增强了决策模块的整体性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的融合图像质量评估与基于置信度学习机制的模仿学习框架，有效应对了图像观测失真对机器人智能控制性能的挑战，提升了决策的可靠性和准确性。

Abstract: The rapid development of intelligent control methodologies has endowed robots with powerful autonomous intelligence. Cable routing, a ubiquitous foundational task in industry, provides a rigorous benchmark for robotic dexterity and sequential decision-making. In these practical scenarios, image observation distortion frequently occurs. Samples characterized by low-quality image observations often hinder accurate model training, posing challenges to the reliability and accuracy of intelligent control systems. Nevertheless, no dedicated intelligent control solution has been proposed for scenarios of image signal distortion. Meanwhile, image quality information has not been sufficiently exploited to further enhance the performance of intelligent control methodologies. To this end, we propose a novel robotic imitation learning framework that comprises an image quality assessment module, a confidence-based learning mechanism, and a decision-making module, which is designed to maintain high performance even under distorted image observations. In the proposed framework, the image quality assessment module synergizes with the confidence-based learning mechanism to enhance the efficacy of the decision-making module. Specifically, the image quality assessment module is incorporated to extract image quality information from image observations, while the confidence-based learning mechanism adaptively prioritizes challenging samples to improve learning effectiveness. The decision-making module determines appropriate discrete skills or continuous actions. Experimental results demonstrate that our formulated framework enhances the overall performance of the decision-making module.

</details>


### [124] [LUCID: Learning Embodiment-Agnostic Intent Models from Unstructured Human Videos for Scalable Dexterous Robot Skill Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11628)
*Harsh Gupta,Guanya Shi,Wenzhen Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为LUCID的两阶段机器人学习框架，它能够从大规模的互联网非结构化人类视频中学习任务意图，并通过一个共享的意图接口，将该意图转换为不同机器人（如灵巧手和平行夹爪）的具体动作，从而实现了在少量数据监督下的现实世界任务执行和跨机器人的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的机器人学习流程依赖于从机器人演示或结构化人类数据中学习技能，这些数据不仅收集成本高昂，而且与特定的机器人实体强绑定，泛化性差。相比之下，互联网上丰富的非结构化人类视频提供了可扩展的替代方案，但一个核心挑战是如何将视频中的操作意图有效地转化为不同机器人的控制动作。

Method: 本文提出了一个两阶段框架：1. 意图模型学习：从互联网视频中，以当前观测为输入，在闭环中预测短期场景意图（即“接下来应该发生什么”）。2. 机器人控制学习：一个特定于实体（机器人形态）的感觉运动策略将共享的意图模型输出转换为具体的机器人动作。其关键设计在于“意图接口”的抽象，使得同一个意图模型可以服务于不同的机器人实体。训练依赖于大规模并行仿真和真实视频数据。

Result: 在五个真实世界的操作任务上评估了LUCID的效果：在仅使用互联网视频监督的“搅拌”、“擦拭”和“分拣”任务中，框架实现了对新场景和物体的零样本迁移；在仅使用1小时自收集智能手机视频监督的“推T”和“布线”任务中，也取得了有效的性能。结果表明，该框架能够利用非结构化视频数据，并通过意图共享机制实现跨实体的控制。

Conclusion: LUCID成功地展示了一条利用大规模、易获取的非结构化人类视频数据进行机器人技能学习的可行路径。通过解耦任务意图与机器人执行，该框架降低了数据收集成本，提高了模型的泛化能力，并实现了跨不同机器人形态的零样本迁移。这为构建更具通用性和可扩展性的机器人学习系统提供了重要的思路。

Abstract: The most widely-adopted robot learning pipelines today learn skills from robot demonstrations or structured human data, which are expensive to collect and tied to specific embodiments. In contrast, unstructured human videos provide a scalable alternative. They contain diverse manipulation demonstrations across objects, scenes, and strategies, but are not directly connected to robot action. We propose LUCID, a two-stage framework that learns task intent from unstructured human videos drawn from internet-scale datasets and learns robot control in massively-parallel simulation. The intent model predicts short-horizon intent (what should happen next in the scene) from the current observation in closed loop. An embodiment-specific sensorimotor policy converts this intent into robot actions. The intent interface is shared across controllers, so the same intent model can be applied to different embodiments, from our primary dexterous hand to a parallel-jaw gripper. We evaluate LUCID on five real-world manipulation tasks: stirring, wiping, and binning supervised by only internet video, with zero-shot transfer to novel scenes and object instances; and push-T and cable routing supervised by 1 hr each of self-collected smartphone video. Project page: https://lucid-robot.github.io/.

</details>


### [125] [SAFER-Nav: Enhancing Safety for Visual Robot Navigation via Segmentation-Aware Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11636)
*Geonyeong Ko,Giung Lee,Changjoo Nam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种改进的视觉导航模型，通过微调将障碍物边界和可通行空间结构直接融入策略中，以提升其在陌生环境中的安全性，同时保持目标到达性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的导航基础模型在面对未知障碍物或环境变化的部署场景时泛化能力不足，生成的轨迹虽能导向目标但安全性欠佳。现有的安全增强方法（如外部轨迹校正或内部几何先验）未能在策略中显式地学习和表征障碍物边界与可通行空间的结构。

Method: 提出一种新型导航模型，通过微调过程将障碍物边界和可通行自由空间的结构信息直接整合到策略中。该方法设计上兼容多种基于RGB的骨干网络。

Result: 在多个机器人平台、多种室内环境以及包含静态和动态障碍物的场景中进行实验验证。结果表明，与ViNT、NoMaD及其CARE增强变体相比，该方法在保持目标到达性能的同时，显著降低了碰撞频率。

Conclusion: 将几何结构信息（障碍物边界与可通行空间）显式地融入视觉导航策略的微调过程，是提升模型在陌生环境中安全性的有效途径，且能保持其泛化与目标到达能力。

Abstract: Vision-based navigation models, particularly foundation models, generate viable trajectories from RGB observations alone. However, even state-of-the-art transformer- and diffusion-based policies struggle to generalize in unfamiliar deployment environments containing unseen obstacles or shifted conditions. The resulting trajectories often remain goal-directed but unsafe. Existing efforts improve safety through external trajectory correction or internal geometric priors, yet the resulting policies are not trained to explicitly represent obstacle boundaries or traversable free-space structure. To address this, we propose a navigation model that incorporates these structures directly into the policy via fine-tuning and is designed to be compatible with diverse RGB-based backbones. Across multiple robot platforms, indoor environments, and static and dynamic obstacle scenarios, our method reduces collision frequency relative to ViNT, NoMaD, and their CARE-augmented variants while maintaining goal-reaching performance.

</details>


### [126] [Improving Human Diving Endurance with a Field-Deployable, Untethered Exoskeleton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11704)
*Zhihao Zhou,Zhenmeng Ju,Rui Yang,Chenxi Zhang,Zhihao Zhou,Ming Xu,Enhao Zheng,Dongjie Jiang,Lecheng Ruan,Jingeng Mai,Qining Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究首次将外骨骼技术应用于水下潜水，研发了名为DiveMate的可部署、无系绳外骨骼。实验表明，该设备能显著提升潜水员的耐力与效率，将给定能量（呼吸气体）下的行进距离提高42.9%，并将潜水时间延长54.9%，同时大幅降低气体消耗率和生理负荷。这标志着外骨骼技术应用领域的重大拓展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类水下潜水的耐力受到克服水阻的高能耗和自携呼吸气体有限的根本限制。虽然外骨骼技术已证明可降低陆地运动的代谢成本，但其在增强水下潜水耐力方面的潜力从未被探索。

Method: 研发并测试了一款名为DiveMate的场部式、无系绳外骨骼。该设备设计用于真实水下环境，能够提供自适应的踢腿辅助。研究人员在自然潜水场景中对其进行了性能评估。

Result: 在自然潜水测试中，DiveMate使潜水员使用给定能量（呼吸气体）的行进距离提高了42.9%。通过降低气体消耗率，它使潜水时间延长了54.9%。肌肉活动显著降低，表明生理负荷减小，净气体消耗率降低了47.0%。运动学特征和规律性的改善进一步证实了其高效的能量经济性。

Conclusion: 应用外骨骼辅助有利于提高人类水下潜水的耐力，增强其探索水下世界的能力。本研究将外骨骼技术的应用前沿拓展至水下领域，并为未来水下辅助设备的设计与评估提供了潜在的参考。

Abstract: Human endurance in underwater locomotion is fundamentally restricted by high energetic demands to overcome drag and the finite supply of self-contained breathing gas. While exoskeleton technology can reduce the metabolic cost of humans in terrestrial locomotion, its potential to enhance human endurance during underwater diving remains entirely unexplored. Here, we present DiveMate, a field-deployable, untethered exoskeleton designed to improve human diving endurance via adaptive kick assistance in real-world underwater environments. During naturalistic diving, DiveMate increases the travel distance using a given energy (breathing gas) by 42.9% and extends dive duration by 54.9% through reducing gas consumption rate. Marked reductions in muscle activation indicate a decrease in physiological exertion, with the net gas consumption rate decreasing by 47.0%. Kinematic characteristics and regularity improvements further underpin efficient energy economy. These results suggest that applying exoskeleton assistance is beneficial for improving human diving endurance and augmenting their ability to explore the aquatic world. This study extends the application frontier of exoskeletons and provides a potential reference for the design and assessment of future underwater assistive devices.

</details>


### [127] [Explore From Sketch: Accelerating UAV Exploration in Large-scale Environments with Prior Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11708)
*Tiancheng Lai,Yuman Gao,Xiangyu Li,Ruitian Pang,Xingpeng Wang,Siqi Shen,Mengke Zhang,Yin He,Fei Gao,Chao Xu,Yanjun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用稀疏、未对齐甚至不一致的2D先验地图进行基于LiDAR的无人机自主探索的新框架，通过鲁棒的2D-3D点云配准和分层视点规划，显著提升了在大规模复杂环境中的探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模、拓扑复杂的环境中，无人机（UAV）的自主探索常因调度次优和路径迂回而效率低下。许多场景中虽有先验地图（如施工图），但它们往往不精确且存在缺陷。本文旨在利用这些先验地图中的全局结构信息来指导探索，以提升效率。

Method: 1. **鲁棒的2D-3D点云配准流程**：结合GeoContext描述符进行单帧候选检索，采用多帧验证机制进行粗配准与离群值剔除，并通过Scale-ICP算法进行精配准。该流程能处理地图差异，并在几何歧义时提供多假设结果。
2. **分层视点规划策略**：在定位不确定性下，首先将局部视点空间关联至先验引导点，并采用蒙特卡洛树搜索求解器在每个配准假设下确定其遍历顺序。为减轻配准不确定性，采用风险感知选择器评估基于置信度加权的出行风险的先验序列，并制定固定端点的旅行商问题以生成高效的局部覆盖路径。

Result: 基准测试表明，与最先进方法相比，探索效率提升了34.2%，飞行距离减少了37.9%。广泛的仿真和实地实验进一步证明了该方法对先验地图不完整和形变具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的框架能够有效利用质量不高的先验地图为无人机自主探索提供全局结构指导，通过创新的配准方法和规划策略，显著提高了探索效率和路径质量，并对先验信息的缺陷表现出强鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration with UAVs in large-scale, topologically complex environments often suffers from low efficiency due to suboptimal scheduling and detours. Prior maps (e.g., construction drawings), although usually imprecise and flawed, are readily available in many scenarios and have the potential to provide global structural guidance. This paper presents a novel exploration framework that leverages sparse, unaligned, and even discrepant 2D prior maps for LiDAR-based UAV exploration. First, a robust 2D-3D point cloud registration pipeline is proposed to align LiDAR observations with prior maps. The registration pipeline combines a GeoContext descriptor for single-frame candidate retrieval, a multi-frame verification mechanism for coarse transformation estimation with outlier rejection, and a Scale-ICP algorithm for refinement. The registration module can handle map discrepancies and provide multiple hypotheses when geometric ambiguities arise. To effectively utilize the registration results for exploration planning, we further develop a hierarchical viewpoint planning strategy under localization uncertainties. The hierarchical strategy first spatially attaches local viewpoints to prior guidepoints and adopts a Monte Carlo Tree Search solver to determine their traversal sequence under each registration hypothesis. To mitigate registration uncertainty, a risk-aware selector evaluates prior sequences using confidence-weighted travel risk, and a fixed-endpoint traveling salesman problem is formulated to generate an efficient local coverage path under the selected prior guidance.
